結論
➢ 結論
AI算力需求推升資料中心擴建，電力與散熱成為建置瓶頸
2030年AI Workload容量上看156GW(CAGR 28.8%)；電網補強需3~5年，機房建置僅需1~2年。
接電時程落差推升現地供電需求，SOFC取得大型AIDC訂單
Oracle、Nebius、AEP/Google相繼採用BE燃料電池；2030年SOFC市場機會約22GW，資料中心占49%。
液冷由晶片延伸至整機與電源，規格與價值量同步升級
Rubin世代全面液冷，CDU放大至2.3~3MW；800VDC電源櫃液冷化，快接頭升級至大口徑與ZQD。
看好液冷冷板、快接頭與SOFC關鍵零組件供應鏈
奇鋐(3017)、富世達(6805)受惠液冷規格升級；高力(8996)切入SOFC供應鏈，宏致(3605)列入觀察。

### Page 4

3
AI 算力需求推升科技業者資本支出，帶動資料中心長期擴建
➢ AI算力需求與AIDC建置瓶頸
預估全球AI Workload容量需求將由2025年44GW成長至2030年156GW，CAGR達28.8%，五年新增112GW。顯示AI算力
需求將持續帶動資料中心建置及相關基礎設施投資。
預估北美主要雲端與科技業者(CSP)資本支出將由2025年4,450億美元成長至2028年1.15兆美元，CAGR達37.2%。隨CSP持
續投入算力擴建，將帶動AI伺服器、機櫃、電力及散熱等基礎設施需求，支撐相關供應鏈中長期成長動能。
AI算力需求驅動資料中心擴建，2030年AI Workload容量上看156GW CSP資本支出加速擴張，2028年規模上看1.15兆美元
資料來源：McKinsey & Company、兆豐投顧整理
GW
資料來源：兆豐投顧預估及整理

### Page 5

4
AIDC 擴建推升用電規模與機櫃密度，供電及散熱能力影響建置進度
➢ AI算力需求與AIDC建置瓶頸
電網擴建與資料中心建置存在時程落差，電力取得速度成為AIDC擴張的重要限制。IEA指出，新建電網從規劃、許可至完工
可能需5–15年，資料中心建置則約1–3年。
AI運算朝高密度系統整合，配電與液冷升級需求涵蓋GPU及CSP自研ASIC。除NVIDIA GB200／GB300外，Google TPU 8t
／8i採第四代液冷，AWS Trainium3 Teton3 MAX採冷板液冷架構，AMD Helios亦已有支援最高246kW雙寬機櫃的合作參
考設計，系統驗證與量產交付能力將成為AIDC建置之關鍵。
全球再生能源與大型儲能併網排隊規模逾2,300 GW 高密度GPU與ASIC機櫃功率上升將持續推動配電及液冷升級
資料來源：IEA、兆豐投顧整理
GW
資料來源：兆豐投顧預估及彙整
kW

### Page 6

5
電網補強與機房建置存在時程落差，提升AIDC現地供電需求
➢ AI算力需求與AIDC建置瓶頸
AIDC擴張需同步取得發電供應、輸配電容量及場址受電能力，任一環節受限，皆可能影響機房投運時程。隨單一場址用電規
模擴大，部分專案需新增變電站、饋線及相關設備，供電進度因而受到重電設備交期拉長、區域電網併網與饋線容量不足、
熱損耗與轉換效率等因素影響。
NLR指出，美國資料中心建置與負載爬坡通常約需12–24個月，公用事業電網補強則常需3–5年。我們認為，兩者的時程落差
將提高業者評估分階段供電及現地發電的意願，並為燃料電池與燃氣機組等方案創造導入機會；實際效益仍須綜合評估燃料、
許可、可靠度與完整供電成本。
AIDC供電涉及多個環節，場址接電能力影響投運時程 資料中心(AIDC)建置與電網補強時程比較

### Page 7

6
接電時程落差推升現地供電需求，SOFC提供模組化連續供電選項
➢ SOFC現地供電與台廠供應鏈
當電網補強時程無法配合機房投運，業者可評估現地發電，搭配分階段送電與儲能等方式銜接供電需求。方案選擇須綜合考
量連續供電能力、燃料供應、建置條件與可靠度要求。
Bloom Energy採用固態氧化物燃料電池(SOFC)，透過電化學反應將燃料轉換為電力，可依需求配置模組，提供持續性現地
電源。其應用可涵蓋與電網並聯或微電網架構，實際部署仍須配合燃料、許可及場內電力系統設計。
現地發電增加機房供電來源 現地發電與儲能方案比較
資料來源：Bloom Energy、兆豐投顧整理 資料來源：DOE、Cummins、Caterpillar、BE、兆豐投顧整理
註：本圖簡化供電路徑，電力轉換、UPS、儲能與備援配置依案場設計
方案 主要功能 選用時的評估重點
電池儲能 電力銜接 與功率調節 儲能量體、輸出功率及充電來源
柴油發電機 停電備援 依額定選擇運轉用途 燃料補給、設備額定及適用許可
燃氣渦輪 可配置為 持續發電來源 天然氣供應與工程、運轉條件

SOFC燃料電池 模組化配置 持續發電 燃料供應、高溫啟停及電堆維護
註：實際用途依額定與案場設計；天然氣SOFC仍有CO2排放

### Page 8

7
SOFC以電化學反應直接發電，模組化整合支援資料中心現地供電
➢ SOFC現地供電與台廠供應鏈
BE採固態氧化物燃料電池(SOFC)，氧離子穿過固態電解質與燃料進行電化學反應直接發電，電子經外部迴路流動，形成直
流電，不經燃燒，電效率達 53–65%。SOFC由多片電池組成電堆，再整合為發電模組，搭配燃料處理、空氣供應、熱管理
與電力轉換設備，形成完整供電系統。
一座1GW的資料中心採用濕式冷卻燃氣渦輪機聯合循環發電系統，在其使用壽命期間，大約會消耗250億加侖的水；但若是
用燃料電池僅需140萬加侖。
氧離子穿越陶瓷電解質，電子經外部迴路形成電力 電池片逐級整合為電堆、發電模組與供電系統
資料來源：IEA、兆豐投顧整理 資料來源：AEP、兆豐投顧整理

### Page 9

8
發電效率與部署條件，決定SOFC在AIDC的採用機會
➢ SOFC現地供電與台廠供應鏈
AIDC持續運轉將累積大量燃料支出，提高發電效率可降低相同電力輸出所需的天然氣量。SOFC具高效率發電特性，其經濟
價值須結合燃料價格、使用時數及維護成本評估。
SOFC、燃氣引擎與燃氣渦輪各有部署及運轉條件。業主須共同評估設備交付、場址工程、燃氣供應、排放許可及維運需求，
才能判斷適合的現地供電方案。
資料來源：兆豐投顧試算 資料來源：DOE、GE、Caterpillar、BE、兆豐投顧整理
相同電力輸出下，提高效率可降低燃料需求 現地供電方案須兼顧效率、擴建與運轉維護

### Page 10

9
政策支援燃料電池投資，木星計畫燃氣與許可牽動落地時程
➢ SOFC現地供電與台廠供應鏈
木星計畫位於美國新墨西哥州，為Oracle支援OpenAI Stargate建置的AI資料中心園區。Oracle於2026年4月宣布，規劃採
用最高2.45GW的Bloom Energy燃料電池裝置容量，取代原先燃氣輪機與柴油發電機方案，展現對SOFC現地供電的需求。
木星計畫的SOFC方案仍以天然氣供應為前提，燃氣接入與空氣品質許可均影響建置進度。州土地辦公室7月維持拒發特定管
線通行權等申請，Oracle於10月另提出微電網設計補件，顯示大型專案須同步協調設備建置、燃料供應與案場許可。
美國 OBBBA 將合格燃料電池納入 Section 48E 投資稅額抵減 甲骨文木星計劃微電網建置須經許可審查與方案調整
資料來源：GPO、兆豐投顧整理 資料來源：NMED、NMSLO、Oracle、兆豐投顧整理
項目 內容
適用制度 Section 48E 合格燃料電池專屬條款
抵減比例 合格投資基礎金額 x 30%

排放條件 專屬條款排除一般設施的溫室氣體排放資格門檻
加成限制 此項30%比例不得疊加其他加成

10
大型AIDC合作案擴大，BE交付與擴產節奏成為供應鏈觀察重點
➢ SOFC現地供電與台廠供應鏈
Oracle、Nebius等業者已公告採用BE燃料電池，顯示SOFC已取得大型AIDC商業合作。評估需求規模時，須分別辨識合作
上限、已簽約容量及實際部署進度。
機組製造、場址工程與許可程序須共同推進。供應鏈需求應依BE製造擴產、設備出貨及案場交付節奏追蹤，並將融資合作框
架與設備訂單分開評估。
BE大型合作案涵蓋設備採購、供電服務與專案融資 BE製造產能隨現地供電需求擴張，2027年起每年增加逾2GW

### Page 12

11
SOFC擴建帶動高溫熱管理與電力線纜需求，台廠切入不同供應環節
➢ SOFC現地供電與台廠供應鏈
依Ceres基於BNEF資料之分析，2030年全球SOFC市場機會約22GW，其中資料中心應用占49%(約10.8GW)，為最大單一
應用市場。隨AI資料中心擴建與供電需求提升，資料中心可望成為SOFC商業化的重要需求來源，帶動高溫熱管理與電力零
組件的應用機會。
SOFC系統需要高溫熱交換、電力轉換與系統配線等零組件。高力提供熱反應器、複熱器等高溫熱管理關鍵零組件；宏致則
供應高溫反應模組耐高溫電纜、電力控制與轉換模組內電纜，以及系統管理與電力傳輸線纜。
Ceres預估2030年全球SOFC市場機會達22GW，其中資料中心占49% 325 kW Energy Server 的組件與運作方式

### Page 13

12
供電與液冷協同升級，支撐AIDC算力密度與能源效率提升
➢ 液冷系統擴張與AIDC效率升級
在總供電及機房空間受限下，AIDC須兼顧能源與空間使用效率。降低冷卻與配電等基礎設施耗能，可在相同總用電條件下提
高IT設備用電占比；評估整體效益時，除PUE外，仍須觀察單位運算耗電及單位面積算力。
液冷透過冷板、分歧管與CDU將熱帶至設施端，高壓直流與Power sidecar則調整供電及設備配置。隨散熱需求延伸至電源
模組，供電與冷卻系統須協同設計，兼顧電力轉換、散熱耗能及部署密度。而電源模組採用液冷，全新的水冷資料中心機房
即不再需要傳統的冷熱通道(Hot/Cold Aisle)與大型空調系統，基礎設施CAPEX預估可節省高達10%。
相同總用電下，降低基礎設施耗能可提高IT用電占比 運算與電源同步液冷，降低對機房氣流散熱的依賴
資料來源：DOE、兆豐投顧試算 資料來源：NVIDIA、MEGMEET、兆豐投顧整理
度/電
註：依PUE定義進行情境試算，非特定液冷方案實測結果。IT設備用

電包含伺服器內部風扇等耗電，不等同晶片運算用電。

### Page 14

13
熱捕捉範圍擴大，液冷設計由主晶片延伸至整機架構
➢ 液冷系統擴張與AIDC效率升級
除CPU、GPU、TPU及ASIC等主要熱源外，液冷進一步涵蓋DIMM、OSFP光模組與板級元件，透過冷板及導熱板提高液體
帶走熱量的比例，降低對氣流散熱的依賴。
為支援不同熱源與機構配置，運算主板與整機伺服器之液冷解決方案多元化，包括冷板迴路式、混合式及整合式大冷板架構。
我們認為，隨液冷涵蓋元件增加，流道與連接設計、模組組裝及可靠度驗證的重要性將提高，供應鏈機會亦由單顆冷板延伸
至整機冷卻整合。
液冷涵蓋主晶片與周邊元件，熱捕捉比例最高達100% 冷板迴路式、混合式與整合式架構支援不同整機配置
資料來源：CoolIT、兆豐投顧整理 資料來源： CoolIT、兆豐投顧整理

### Page 15

14
CDU串接IT與設施冷卻迴路，承接液冷擴張後的排熱需求
➢ 液冷系統擴張與AIDC效率升級
隨液冷由CPU、GPU延伸至記憶體、光模組及板級元件，液體承擔的設備熱量增加；機櫃功率密度提高，更進一步推升液冷
迴路的排熱需求。CDU串接IT側與設施側，透過熱交換器、循環泵浦與控制系統維持供液條件，其換熱能力與供液穩定性將
成為高密度AIDC的重要設計環節。
CDU升級除提高散熱容量，更須兼顧能源與空間使用效率。OCP 2026的2.3MW系統模擬研究顯示，在相同散熱負載下，比
較方案間全年冷卻系統能耗差距約17%。
冷板取熱、CDU換熱、設施端排熱，構成完整液冷迴路 相同2.3MW散熱負載之下，CDU選型仍牽動流量與全年能耗

註：典型液對液CDU示意圖。紅、藍代表各迴路相對高低溫，非特定
案場溫度。

### Page 16

15
Deschutes開放2MW級CDU設計，瞄準能源與空間效率提升
➢ 液冷系統擴張與AIDC效率升級
Google將第五代CDU設計Deschutes貢獻予OCP，提供高密度AI機房可採用的開放規格。其v1.0設計以2MW散熱容量、
3°C接近溫差及IT側與設施側各500GPM流量為目標，透過三組雙流程熱交換器並聯，兼顧換熱表現與流阻，支撐高熱負載液
冷部署。依規格本體尺寸換算，Deschutes占地約1.78平方公尺，散熱容量密度約1.12MW／平方公尺；搭配N+1無軸封泵
浦及溫度、壓力與流量監控，兼顧容量與供液可靠度。我們認為，CDU的競爭重點將涵蓋單位占地容量、低溫差換熱及泵浦
耗能，須從整體冷卻系統評估其能源與空間效益。
熱交換器、泵浦與控制整合等關鍵組件，支撐MW級CDU穩定運轉 2MW容量與3°C接近溫差，兼顧高熱負載與緊湊配置

800VDC重整供電配置，液冷延伸至高密度電源櫃
➢ 液冷系統擴張與AIDC效率升級
隨AIDC機櫃功率提高，低電壓供電面臨大電流、導體體積與電源設備占用空間等挑戰。Power sidecar將前端電力轉換與部
分備援設備移至獨立電源櫃，再以800VDC送至運算櫃，並依架構於負載附近降壓；透過提高輸電電壓與調整設備配置，支
援更高功率密度部署。
NVIDIA第二代MGX Power Rack參考設計，朝100%液冷發展，並標示880kW供電能力(N配置、含BBU)及90秒備援供電時
間。隨電力轉換與備援設備集中，液冷需求將進一步延伸至電源模組，使供電與散熱成為能源及空間效率升級的重要環節。
前端電源移至獨立機櫃，800VDC支援高功率密度供電 NVIDIA MGX第二代電源櫃朝全液冷發展，液冷散熱模組與零組件用量提升
資料來源：NVIDIA、MEGMEET、兆豐投顧整理 資料來源：NVIDIA、兆豐投顧整理
註：圖示為保留54V櫃內供電的過渡架構。整體空間效益須連同電源
櫃占地評估。

### Page 18

17
液冷導入電源模組，推進功率密度與能源效率升級
➢ 液冷系統擴張與AIDC效率升級
電源模組導入液冷後，可透過冷板將功率元件的熱量帶至冷卻液，降低對風扇、大型散熱鰭片與風道的需求。相較風冷設計，
液冷有助縮小散熱結構占用空間，並降低風扇耗電及元件工作溫度，使電源模組朝更高功率密度發展。
MEGMEET於2026年10月OCP展示100kW、800V液冷AC/DC電源層，採2RU不含BBU設計，產品標示轉換效率逾98%；另
展示1RU、95kW的800V轉54V液冷DC/DC電源層。我們認為，液冷應用延伸至整流與降壓環節，將擴大電源冷板、流道設
計及模組整合的需求，提升水冷板、快接頭、滑軌、機殼與液冷busbar之需求量。
水冷板承接功率元件熱量，降低電源模組對風扇與風道的依賴 液冷電源層涵蓋前端整流與櫃內降壓，支援緊湊供電配置
資料來源：NVIDIA、MEGMEET、兆豐投顧整理 資料來源： MEGMEET、兆豐投顧整理

晶片TDP與出貨量同步提升，水冷板技術與ASP仍具升級空間
➢ 液冷系統擴張與AIDC效率升級
晶片TDP逐代提升，NVIDIA由GB300的1.4kW增至Rubin 2.3kW、Rubin Ultra約2.6kW，TPU v9與Trainium4將達3~4kW。
我們認為，至少至Rubin Ultra世代，3kW以內熱負載仍可由目前冷板架構解熱，並透過微通道、雙面水冷板(4→6kW)及鑽
石銅等材料升級持續解熱。
我們預估AI晶片出貨量由2025年1,036萬顆增至2027年2,403萬顆(26/27年YoY+39%/+67%)，其中ASIC於2027年達1,333萬
顆、占比由37%升至55%，超越GPU；出貨放量加上電源櫃與光模組導入液冷，水冷板用量與滲透率仍有提升空間。
TDP持續攀升，水冷板技術與ASP仍有升級空間 AI晶片出貨量預估，整體供應鏈於2027年仍供不應求

冷板與流體零組件升級，須兼顧散熱效能與系統耗電
➢ 液冷系統擴張與AIDC效率升級
晶片功耗提升推高冷板流量需求，惟CDU可供IT側使用的壓差有限(Deschutes規格為80~90psi)，冷板與快接頭須以更低壓
降輸送更大流量。Rubin Ultra將導入內建球閥、提升流量的ZQD；我們認為，快接頭技術門檻由防漏延伸至大流量低壓降，
帶動規格與單價升級。
NVIDIA MGX第二代電源櫃全面液冷，以1座電源櫃搭配2座運算櫃為一組試算，每組新增26片電源冷板及52顆快接頭；惟兩
段電源轉換熱損合計僅約供電量4%，增量以件數為主，價值量約為運算櫃快接頭的5%。
電源設備液冷化：每組(1*電源櫃+2*運算櫃)零組件用量試算 流量提升推動快接頭規格升級，Rubin Ultra每tray 10顆MQD改採ZQD

約US$30~40；UQD04無實際報價，以小口徑QD均價估計；ZQD最終價格未定。熱負載以轉換效率約98%估算；AC/DC每層以880kW÷8層計。

每櫃液冷價值隨世代提升，水冷板與快接頭量價齊揚
➢ 液冷系統擴張與AIDC效率升級
Compute Tray為液冷價值主體：每層水冷板由VR200約1,500美元升至Rubin Ultra約2,000美元(可能導入雙面設計)；快接

顯提升。

液冷由晶片延伸至整機與電源，單櫃價值倍增帶動台廠受惠
➢ 液冷系統擴張與AIDC效率升級
液冷由CPU、GPU延伸至記憶體、光模組及電源設備，Rubin世代全面採用100%液冷，CDU容量放大至2.3~3MW。我們預
估，單櫃液冷價值(含CDU)由約10萬美元倍增至約20萬美元，全球液冷市場將由2026年105億美元成長至2030年853億美元
(CAGR約69%)。
價值升級沿三條主線：CDU放大容量與泵浦冗餘、冷板導入雙面水冷與鑽石銅、快接頭朝大口徑低壓降及ZQD升級；電源櫃
液冷則以件數貢獻增量。我們認為，冷板與快接頭兼具規格升級與用量成長，奇鋐、雙鴻、富世達最直接受惠。
全球液冷市場2030年達853億美元，二次側占約7成5 液冷各環節升級方向與單櫃價值量占比

### Page 23

22
結論
➢ 結論
AI算力需求推升資料中心擴建，電力與散熱成為建置瓶頸
2030年AI Workload容量上看156GW(CAGR 28.8%)；電網補強需3~5年，機房建置僅需1~2年。
接電時程落差推升現地供電需求，SOFC取得大型AIDC訂單
Oracle、Nebius、AEP/Google相繼採用BE燃料電池；2030年SOFC市場機會約22GW，資料中心占49%。
液冷由晶片延伸至整機與電源，規格與價值量同步升級
Rubin世代全面液冷，CDU放大至2.3~3MW；800VDC電源櫃液冷化，快接頭升級至大口徑與ZQD。
看好液冷冷板、快接頭與SOFC關鍵零組件供應鏈
奇鋐(3017)、富世達(6805)受惠液冷規格升級；高力(8996)切入SOFC供應鏈，宏致(3605)列入觀察。

### Page 2

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
 美國期中選舉由民主黨贏得參議院及眾議院多數席次，川普剩餘兩年任內將面
臨分立政府的局面，市場擔憂民主黨未來傾向加強 AI監管，恐削弱美國經濟成
長動能。
 歐元區 10月 CPI年增率自前月的 3.8%上升至 4%，創下三年來的新高，通膨面
臨上行風險。歐洲央行管委暨斯洛伐克央行總裁卡日米爾(Peter Kazimir)表示，
歐洲央行將持續升息，以防止高通膨根深蒂固；德國央行總裁納格爾(Joachim
Nagel)亦表示通膨前景尚未改善，歐洲央行應於 12 月升息。多位歐洲央行官員
發表偏鷹言論，市場預期歐洲央行未來一年內將再升息二至三次，升息預期升
溫，預計將支撐歐元走勢。
 美國 10 月 ISM 製造業 PMI 為 55.5，高於市場預期，製造業擴張速度加快；另
10 月 ISM 非製造業 PMI為 54.5，持穩於景氣榮枯線之上，顯示美國整體基本面
佳，預計將為美元提供支撐。

 美國 10 月非農就業人數增加 16.5 萬人，優於市場預期的 6.5 萬人，另 10 月失
業率亦由前月的 4.2%降至 4.1%，顯示勞動市場仍具韌性，在美國經濟穩健及通
膨維持高位的情況下，強化聯準會 12月升息預期。
 澳洲央行於 11/3 將現金基準利率調升一碼至 4.85%，係今年以來第五次升息並
創下 2008 年 11 月以來新高水準。然而，此次九名委員中有三人主張維持利率
不變，投票結果出現分歧，加上貨幣政策委員會聲明中，刪除有關可能進一步升
息的明確表述，令市場預期澳洲央行將於 12月暫停升息。

### Page 3

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Nov/09/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週台幣匯價高點為 32.500，低點在 33.040，以 32.768 作收，較前一週收盤價
貶值 2 角 9 分。 
    上週台幣走貶，週初，台股開低，市場對 AI投資支出及科技股估值的疑慮，致
使外資連續多日賣超台股並匯出，台幣連貶數日，加以週間美國期中選舉結果出爐，
民主黨勝選，市場擔憂 AI產業監管趨嚴，台股重挫，外資上週累計賣超台股 5,244
億，外資強勢匯出之下，儘管逢高有出口商拋匯抑制台幣貶值速度，台幣仍貶破 33
整數大關，一度觸及年內低點 33.040，惟週五午盤過後，外資及壽險轉向匯入，加
以出口商拋匯需求，台幣跌幅收斂，終場在央行調節之下收在 32.768。

＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週台幣走勢偏弱。就國際市場而言，美國非農就業數據意外高於預期、
通膨壓力仍在，強化市場對於聯準會 12月升息預期，高利率將有助國際美元走升；
就台灣市場來看，市場對 AI產業資本支出、融資成本及信用風險產生疑慮，加以民
主黨勝選，引發市場對 AI監管政策收緊與資料中心建設放緩的擔憂，外資持續賣超
台股，令台幣持續面臨貶值的壓力，惟出口商拋匯需求待消化，將抑制台幣貶值速
度，綜合以上，預估本週台幣走勢偏弱。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：32.600-33.200

### Page 4

4  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌人民幣匯率走勢  
Nov/09/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣先升後貶。週初，國際美元偏弱震盪，人民幣受益走升，境內人民
幣相隔四年多首次升破 6.65整數大關；惟週二中國央行宣布調升外匯存款準備金率，
顯示其抑制單邊升值預期的意圖，人民幣大幅走貶；週間，市場持續消化人行政策
影響，人民幣走貶，最低一度貶至 6.6855，不過美國期中選舉結果出爐，市場不確
定性下降，國際油價、美債殖利率及美元指數皆下跌，緩解人民幣的賣壓，惟中國
國有大行逢低持續買進美元，人民幣反彈力道有限，終場境外人民幣收在 6.6736，
週線貶值 212 點，境內人民幣則收在 6.6763，週線貶值 203 點。

＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週人民幣區間震盪。就國際市場來看，美國非農就業人數新增 16.5 萬
人，就業率下降至 4.1%，勞動力市場意外強勁，強化市場對於聯準會 12 月再次升
息的預期，利差因素支撐美元指數反彈，將抑制人民幣走升；就中國國內市場來看，
人行最新政策顯示其抑制人民幣單邊升值的意圖，惟第四季為結匯旺季，出口商實
質拋匯需求部分緩解人民幣貶值壓力；最後就技術面來看，USD/CNH 日線圖中，
USD/CNH重返五日均線之上，顯示人民幣強勢升值的趨勢暫歇，綜合以上，預估本
週人民幣區間震盪。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6600-6.6900

5  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Nov/09/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週日圓走弱。週初，市場等待美國期中選舉結果，交投偏謹慎觀望，USD/JPY
在 158 附近波動；週間，美國期中選舉由民主黨取得參眾議院多數席次，政治僵局
擔憂令美元走弱，USD/JPY最低來到 157.39；臨近週末，美國公布就業數據優於市
場預期，USD/JPY止跌回升，終場收在 158.45，週線上漲 100 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、日本央行於季度展望報告中
表示，日本近期實際利率上升，基礎通膨率已加速升至 2%附近，加上日本央行近期
亦強調政策已進入避免通膨超標為重點的新階段，進一步強化了其鷹派立場，市場
預期日本最快將於 12月再次升息，預計將支撐日圓走升。二、美國 10月 ISM製造
業 PMI 為 55.5，優於市場預期，顯示製造業擴張速度加快；另外，美國公布 10 月
非農就業人數增加 16.5 萬人，優於市場預期的 6.5 萬人，美國經濟強勁、勞動市場
仍具韌性，強化聯準會 12月升息預期，美元持穩，預計將有利 USD/JPY走勢。三、
日本財務大臣片山皋月重申美日聯合匯率聲明仍具效力，將於必要時採取外匯干預
行動，以維護外匯市場有序運作，將限縮 USD/JPY上行空間。四、就技術面而言，
在日線圖中，USD/JPY短中天期均線趨平，近期大致在 157 至 158.50 間盤整，匯價
暫無明確方向性。綜合以上，預估 USD/JPY本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較高。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌日幣：157.00-159.50

美元兌歐元匯率走勢  
             Nov/09/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
  上週 EUR/USD 先上後下。週初，在美伊傳出有望和談消息後，歐元自低點小
幅走升至 1.1237；週間，美國民主黨於期中選舉大獲全勝，川普選舉失利，美元偏
弱震盪；臨近週末，美國公布非農就業人數高於市場預期，歐元自高點回落，終場
收在 1.1143，週線下跌 12點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD震盪偏弱，主要原因如下：一、歐元區 10月 CPI年增率自
前月的 3.8%上升至 4%，創下三年來的新高，通膨面臨上行風險。此外，歐洲央行
管委暨斯洛伐克央行總裁卡日米爾(Peter Kazimir)表示，歐洲央行將持續升息，以防
止高通膨根深蒂固，並表示目前主要考量已非是否需採取進一步行動，而是須衡量
應升息幾次方能將通膨拉回 2%目標水準，市場預期歐洲央行未來一年內將再升息
二至三次，升息預期升溫，預計將支撐歐元走勢。二、美國公布 10月非農就業人數
增加 16.5 萬人，優於市場預期的 6.5 萬人，另 10 月失業率亦由前月的 4.2%降至
4.1%，顯示勞動市場仍具韌性，強化了聯準會 12月升息預期，美元再度走升至 102，
預計將限縮歐元走升空間。三、技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD各天期均線向
下發散，近期匯價多次向上測試 1.1270 均拉回留下長上影線，顯示上方仍有賣壓待
消化。綜合以上，預估 EUR/USD本週呈現震盪偏弱的可能性較高。

＜預估本週＞ 歐元兌美元：1.1030-1.1250

7  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
                           新台幣利率走勢分析

115.11.02-115.11.06 
銀行隔夜拆款利率 0.805% ~ 0.825%  
30天期短票利率   1.67% ~ 1.69% 
公債－10 年期-A15109R 1.885% 
－ 5年期- A15108 1.725%

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分，受台股重挫影響，令週五資金需求上揚，但提存期初市場
資金充裕，短率持穩於近期區間。 
週二央行標售 364天期定存單 1100億元，加權平均得標利率為 1.577%，低於
市場預期區間 1.59%-1.61%，較上次標售結果下跌 2.5個基本點。 
週五隔夜拆款利率區間在 0.805%~0.825%， 30 天期短票主要成交在
1.67%~1.69%，利率較上週上揚 1 個基本點。 
二、 本週市場交投維持冷清，10 年期指標券 A15109R及 5年期指標券 A15108均
未成交，週五等殖系統參考報價中價分別為 1.885%、1.725%。 
股市承壓帶動避險需求，美債利率下滑，令先前台債偏空氛圍緩解，週三標售
30年期公債得標利率 2.267%，低於市場預期區間 2.27%~2.30%，投標倍數 1.85
倍，較上次 1.55 倍提升，不過考量台灣年底仍有升息可能，將抑制買盤向下
追價意願，預估台債利率區間盤整，預估 5 年券區間為 1.70%~1.75%，10 年
券區間為 1.85%~1.90%。

予測

１. 11/16に内閣府から公表される 2026年 7-9月期の実質 GDPは、前期比▲0.1％（前期比年
率▲0.3％）と 4四半期ぶりのマイナス成長になったと推計される。 
２. 高水準の企業収益を背景に設備投資が前期比 1.4％と大幅に増加したが、天候不順の影響
などから民間消費が前期比▲0.3％と減少したこと、原油を中心に輸入が大幅に増加し、
外需が成長率を大きく押し下げたことなどから、小幅ながら 4四半期ぶりのマイナス成
長になったとみられる。 
３. 名目 GDPは前期比 0.5％（前期比年率 2.0％）と 10四半期連続で増加し、実質 GDPの伸
びを大きく上回るだろう。GDPデフレーターは前期比 0.6％、前年比 2.5％と予測する。 
４. 2026年 7-9月期のマイナス成長の一因となった民間消費の減少は、天候不順による一時
的な影響が大きい。ただし、こうした影響が解消されても、物価上昇の加速が消費の回
復を妨げる可能性が高い。 
５. 現時点では、2026年 10-12月期はプラス成長に復帰するものの、民間消費が伸び悩むこ
となどから、前期比年率 0％台半ばとマイナス成長の後としては低めの伸びにとどまる
と予想している。 
ニッセイ基礎研究所 2026-10-30

2026年 7-9月期の実質 GDP 
～前期比▲0.1％（年率▲0.3％）を予測～

経済研究部 経済調査部長 斎藤  太郎 
(03)3512-1836  tsaito@nli-research.co.jp

### Page 2

名目 GDPは前期比 0.5％（前期比年率 2.0％）と 10四半期連続で増加し、実質 GDPの伸びを大
きく上回るだろう。GDPデフレーターは前期比 0.6％（4-6月期：同 1.0％）、前年比 2.5％（4-6月
期：同 2.6％）と予測する。

なお、11/16に内閣府から 2026年 7-9月期の GDP速報が発表される際には、基礎統計の改定や季
節調整のかけ直しなどから、成長率が過去に遡って改定される。当研究所では、2026年 4-6月期の
実質 GDP成長率は、外需、設備投資が上方修正されることから、前期比年率 1.4％から同 2.3％へ
上方修正されると予想している。

2026年 7-9月期のマイナス成長の一因となった民間消費の減少は、天候不順による一時的な影響
が大きい。ただし、こうした影響が解消されても、物価上昇の加速が消費の回復を妨げる可能性が
高い。消費者物価上昇率は 2026年 9月に 9ヵ月ぶりの 2％台となったが、原材料価格の上昇を耐久
財や日用品などの販売価格に転嫁する動きが今後さらに広がることから、2026年度末にかけて 3％
台まで高まる公算が大きい。物価上昇率の高まりによる実質購買力の低下を背景に 2026年度後半
の民間消費は弱めの動きが続くことが予想される。 
現時点では、2026年 10-12月期はプラス成長に復帰するものの、民間消費が伸び悩むことなどか
ら、前期比年率 0％台半ばとマイナス成長の後としては低めの伸びにとどまると予想している。

1 10/30までに公表された経済指標をもとに予測している。今後公表される経済指標の結果によって予測値を修正する可
能性がある。

### Page 3

民間消費は前期比▲0.3％と予測する。 
実質賃金はプラスの伸びが続いているが、物価上昇率の高まりによって実質購買力が押し下げら
れていることに加え、台風や豪雨、長雨などの天候不順が個人消費を下押しした。 
2026年 7-9月期の消費関連指標を確認すると、延べ宿泊者数（うち日本人）は前期比 2.8％（4-6
月期：同▲0.9％）の増加となったが、自動車税の
「環境性能割」の廃止を受けて 4-6月期に前期比
10.7％の高い伸びとなった自動車販売台数が、その
反動で同▲7.5％と落ち込んだほか、外食産業売上
高が前期比▲0.3％（4-6月期：同 0.2％）、小売業
販売額指数が前期比▲2.1％（4-6月期：同 0.6％）
と減少した（いずれもニッセイ基礎研究所による
季節調整値、外食産業売上高、小売業販売額指数
は消費者物価指数で実質化）。

・住宅投資～弱めの動きが続く～

住宅投資は前期比▲0.2％と 2四半期連続の減
少を予測する。 
新設住宅着工戸数（季節調整済・年率換算値）
は、建築物省エネ法・建築基準法改正前の駆け
込み需要の反動で、2025年 1-3月期の 86.7万戸
から 4-6月期に 62.0万戸へ急減した後、7-9月
が 71.9万戸と持ち直し、その後は概ね 70万戸
台半ばで推移している。 
GDP統計の住宅投資は住宅価格上昇の影響も
あり弱めの動きとなっている。

・民間設備投資～高水準の企業収益を背景に増加～

民間設備投資は前期比 1.4％と 3四半期ぶりの増加を予測する。 
設備投資の一致指標である資本財総供給は 2026年 4-6月期の前期比 4.9％の後、7-9月期も同 4.9％
となった。また、機械投資の先行指標である機械受注（船舶・電力を除く民需）は 2026年 4-6月
期に前期比 0.2％と 3四半期連続で増加した後、7、8月の平均は 4-6月期を 0.4％上回っている。 
日銀短観 2026年 9月調査では、2026年度の設備投資計画（含むソフトウェア・研究開発投資額、
除く土地投資額）が 6月調査から 0.5％上方修正され、前年度比 9.3％（全規模・全産業）となった。 
設備投資は、高水準の企業収益を背景に、人手不足対応の省力化投資、デジタル化に向けた情報

・公的固定資本形成～2四半期連続の減少～

公的固定資本形成は、前期比▲2.4％と予測する。 
公共工事の先行指標である公共工事請負金額
は 2026年 7-9月期に前年比▲2.6％と 6四半期ぶ
りに減少した。また、公共工事の進捗を反映する
公共工事出来高（建設総合統計）は、2026年 4-6
月期に前年比 5.2％と 9四半期連続で増加した後、
2026年 7、8月の平均は同 2.4％と伸びが鈍化し
ている。 
2025年度補正予算による押し上げ効果が弱ま
っていることに加え、2026 年度当初予算の公共
事業関係費の伸びが抑えられていることから、弱
めの動きとなっている。

・外需～4四半期ぶりのマイナス寄与～

外需寄与度は前期比▲0.4％（前期比年率▲1.8％）と 4四半期ぶりのマイナスを予測する。財貨・
サービスの輸出が前期比 2.0％、財貨・サービスの輸入が同 4.8％となるだろう。代替調達の進展に
伴う原油輸入量の急増を主因として輸入が高い伸
びとなり、輸出の伸びを上回ったことから、外需は
成長率の押し下げ要因となった。 
2026年 7-9月期の地域別輸出数量指数を季節調整
値（当研究所による試算値）でみると、米国向けが
前期比 0.9％（4-6月期：同 2.8％）、EU向けが前期
比▲2.4％（4-6月期：同 0.3％）、アジア向けが前期
比▲0.3％（4-6 月期：同▲0.8％）、うち中国向けが

Document 102: 261108_citi_MSI

Source PDF: all report pdf/261108_citi_MSI.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-08
Pages: 14
Status: ok

### Page 1

Micro-Star (2377.TW)
3Q26 Preview: Margins to Stay Elevated on Cost Pass-Through; Raise 
TP to NT$140, Stay Neutral
CITI'S TAKE 
MSI will report 3Q26 results in mid-Nov. We now expect net profit of 
NT$3.19bn and EPS of NT$3.77, 18% above con estimates, as 3Q26 GPM 
stays elevated at 15.8% vs. our previous 12.0% estimates, supported by 
better VGA supply and continued ASP pass-through of rising memory 
costs. For 4Q26E, we expect MB/VGA shipments to ease QoQ but ASPs to 
rise, with server contribution to further increase. Looking into 2027E, we 
expect PC demand to remain weak and pass-through room to narrow as 
memory price increases pace slows down. We raise 2026E-28E earnings by 
10-57% and lift our TP to NT$140 (from NT$118; 12x 2027E EPS). We stay 
Neutral, with likely limited upside given uncertain PC recovery and modest 
server contribution. 
3Q26 preview — MSI reported 3Q26 revenue of NT$56.9bn (+14% QoQ/-1% YoY), 
broadly in line with Citi’s forecast and slightly ahead of cons. While Sep MB and VGA 
shipments both exceeded expectations, as VGA supply improved. With memory and 
other raw material prices continuing to rise and MSI able to pass costs through, we 
expect 3Q26 GPM to stay elevated at 15.8%, well above our previous 12.0% estimate. 
Assuming only a modest increase in opex ratio, we forecast net profit of NT$3.19bn 
(+6% QoQ/+88% YoY) and EPS of NT$3.77, 18% above cons forecasts.
Price hike continues — We expect 4Q26E shipments to decline from 3Q26’s peak 
season. Mgmt. also indicated that Oct shipment could go down from Sep high base. 
However, we raise our 4Q26E pricing expectations and believe ASP should stay 
elevated, as memory prices keep climbing and MSI continues to pass costs through. 
The long-cultivated server business should also contribute higher than previous 
quarters. Looking into 2027, PC demand should remain weak as component costs 
stay high; we expect MSI’s MB/VGA/PC shipments to decline by double digits, with 
pass-through room narrowing as the price hike pace slows down.
Implications — We raise our 2026E-28E earnings forecasts by 10-57%, reflecting 
sustained strong component cost pass-through. We lift our TP to NT$140 (from 
NT$118), implying 12x 2027E EPS (rolled over from 12x 2026E EPS). We believe the 
share price already reflects MSI’s ability to pass costs through to end products, and 
we see limited upside, given that the PC demand recovery remains highly uncertain 
and the contribution from the new server business is still limited. Stay Neutral.

### Page 6

Micro-Star (2377.TW)
09 November 2026 Citi Research
6
Micro-Star
Company description
Established in 1986 and listed in 1998, Taiwan-based MSI is a leading
OBM/ODM for gaming PCs, motherboards, graphic cards, industrial PCs, and
servers. MSI started business as a motherboard manufacturer but
transformed itself to become a leading gaming PC play in 2012 with a
comprehensive product portfolio across the gaming system and components.
MSI enjoys about 16% global share in the gaming NB industry, and is ranked

No.3 in graphic card shipments and No.3 in motherboard shipments globally.
Investment strategy
We rate MSI shares at Neutral as we anticipate earnings upward pressure
from the delince in PC and MB/VGA shipments in the near-term. While the
soaring component costs could be partially offset by price hike, we expect
muted demand and AI resource crowding to wiegh on margins upside. Further
upside maintains limited, though, until we see a meaningful recovery in core

gaming demand or a more substantial contribution from the server business.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$140 is based on 12x 2027E EPS, below the stock's five-
year average forward 12-month PE. This valuation reflects the earnings
downside risks from muted PC demand and margin pressure from soaring
component costs, while accounting for the time required for the server
business to achieve meaningful scale.
Risks
Key upside risks that could cause MSI's shares to trade above our target price

include: 1) a sharper-than-expected recovery in high-end gaming demand;
and 2) faster-than-anticipated revenue contribution from the AI server
segment. Key downside risks include: 1) a deeper-than-expected contraction
in global PC shipments; 2) prolonged supply shortages for gaming GPUs due
to Ai resource crowding; and 3) inability to pass through surging memory and
CPU costs to price-sensitive consumers.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

Hon Hai Precision (2317.TW)

CPO: New growth opportunities from vertical integration — Hon Hai sees
significant long-term opportunities in both ASIC and vertical integration including
optical interconnect technologies. In optical networking, management believes the
transition from electrical to optical interconnects is structurally inevitable as AI
models expand and memory-bandwidth requirements increase. Hon Hai’s CPO

optical switching already starts mass volume production for its major customer,
and its micron-level precision manufacturing expertise in optical alignment,
connectors, and electrical-to-optical conversion could create additional value
through component integration and higher system-level content. Its extensive
engineering resources, ODM/JDM development capabilities, and vertically
integrated manufacturing platform should strengthen its competitive positioning.

Management expressed strong confidence in optical growth opportunities but did
not provide specific 2027 revenue guidance.

Robust financials and earnings growth, maintain Buy — Thanks to strong
demand from major CSP clients and its continuous automation and vertical
integration development, Hon Hai’s capex increased 43% YTD. Nevertheless, its
EBITDA increased 50.3% YoY to NT$369.2bn. To improve capital efficiency, Hon

Hai plans to expand consignment arrangements, optimize inventory and
receivables management, and carefully evaluate investment payback periods.
Management reiterated its long-term operating margin floor of above 3%, with
further potential upside from scale economies, automation, product mix
improvements, and greater vertical integration.
Given Hon Hai’s solid result and better operational leverage, we raise our
2026/2027/2028 EPS by 4%/5%/5%. Considering its solid execution, stable

dividend yield and attractive valuation, we reiterate our Buy rating with TP NT$400
unchanged. Our TP implies 15x of our 2027 EPS projection.

Hon Hai Precision (2317.TW)

9
Hon Hai Precision
Company description
Established in 1974, Hon Hai has become the largest EMS player in the world
in terms of sales. Through its vertical integration model or "eCMMS" (e-
enabled Components, Modules, Moves and Services), it provides
components, modules and system assembly products to many global OEMs
in the PC, communication and consumer sectors.
Investment strategy
We rate Hon Hai shares as Buy as we believe the company’s margins will

continue to improve, backed by a renewed focus on profitability, operational
efficiency and new businesses development. We think there’s more upside to
go from current level, as the underlying drivers are not just about product
cycle, but also about fundamental transformation and new business
opportunities.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$400 is based on 15x average 2026E-27E EPS, which is
at the higher end of the historical trading range. We believe this multiple is

justified by the company’s enhanced sales growth and profit margin outlook,
as well as new endeavors into AI server, EV, digital health and robotics. Hon
Hai has been able to generate positive earnings streams, and thus we believe
a P/E valuation methodology is appropriate.
Risks
Downside risks that could impede the stock from reaching our target price
include: 1) slower-than-expected iPhone demand; 2) intensified peer
competition; 3) weaker-than-expected execution on new business progress;

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

M
Update
Lotes Co. Ltd  | Asia Pacific
3Q GM Decline Eases; 4Q
Recovery in Focus
3Q results:
• GM came in at 44.8% (-60bps q/q, -640bps y/y), 20bps above our estimate.
3Q GM continued to face margin headwinds from rising raw material costs,
which we highlighted in our recent upgrade report here. We look forward to
management's comment during its results call to see if 4Q margin will be
guided to return to growth.
• Opex was  higher than expected at NT$2.2bn, 10% above our estimate and

9% above the Street.
• So OpM wa only 22.9% (+100bps q/q, -700bps y/y), 180bps below our
estimate and 250bps below the Street.
• Net profit was NT$1,919mn (-3% q/q, -17% y/y) for EPS of NT$17.09, 12%
below our estimate and 12% below the Street.
• Lotes is holding an earnings call on November 13 at 4 PM HKT; we will
provide takeaways after the call.

Key Takeaways
3Q GM fell 60bps q/q, to 44.8%, 20bps above our estimate but 70bps below the
Street. The sequential decline continued to narrow.

EPS of NT$17.09 missed our estimate and the Street by 12%, with opex 10%
above our estimate/9% above the Street.
We believe the new server CPU socket production ramp in 4Q can support our
view of 4Q GM recovery.
The call tomorrow should help clarify that and the opex overshoot.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
Valuation Methodology and Risks
Lotes Co. Ltd. (3533.TW)
Primary - Residual Income
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions include a cost of equity of 9.6% (beta of
1.0, equity risk premium of 8.7% and risk-free rate of 1.0%), a medium-term growth rate of
12.5%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected growth in the server business, such as from cloud and enter-
prise in China

n Stronger-than-expected DT demand
n Sooner-than-expected inventory digestion of NB
n Faster ramp-up of its EV business
Risks to Downside
n Further deceleration of server demand from US hyperscalers
n Weaker-than-expected DT demand
n Slower-than-expected inventory digestion of NB
n More severe pricing erosion driven by competition

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

SK 하이닉스 (000660/KS) 
여전히 강력하다 
3Q26 영업이익 83 조원으로 예상 상회 
SK하이닉스의 3Q26 영업이익은 83조원 (+38% QoQ)으로 예상을 상회했다. 메모
리 공급 부족 심화, HBM4 반영 시작에 따른 메모리 가격 상승이 환율 하락을 상쇄했
기 때문이다. 출하 DRAM +10%, NAND -1%, 가격 DRAM +28%, NAND 
+24%, 영업이익률 DRAM 82%, NAND 72%, HBM 판가는 HBM4 출하가 시작되
며 37% 수준 상승한 것으로 추정된다. 업황은 여전히 예상을 상회하고 있다.

4Q26 분기 영업이익 100 조원 시대 진입 전망 
4Q26 영업이익은 105조원 (+27% QoQ)으로 분기 100조원 시대에 진입할 것으로 
전망한다. 메모리 가격 강세가 지속되는 가운데, 1c 출하 가속화, M15X 램프업 본격화 
등으로 출하량 상승이 기존 예상을 상회할 것이다. 또한 HBM4 의 Cross-over 시작 
및 수율 안정화로 HBM 가격, 수익성 제고 동반이 지속될 것이다. 출하는 DRAM 
+24%, NAND +4%, 가격은 DRAM +8%, NAND +7%를 예상한다.

2027 년 영업이익 488 조원 (+70% YoY)으로 상향조정 
SK 하이닉스의 2027년 영업이익을 488 조원 (+70% YoY)으로 12% 상향 조정한
다. 원/달러 환율 전망치를 기존 1,340 원에서 1,315 원으로 하향 조정했음에도, 공급 
부족 심화 속 2027년향 HBM 가격 인상 및 예상을 상회하는 수율, 1Q27 진행될 모바
일 DRAM 가격 인상 등을 반영했기 때문이다. LTA 가격 역시 개별 약정된 범위 내 
시장 상황을 반영할 수 있는 구조라는 점을 감안하면, 지속 상승할 것이라 전망한다.

높은 주주환원 의지 확인, 높아진 실적 가시성, 지나친 저평가 
투자의견 매수, 목표주가 4,000,000원을 유지한다. SK하이닉스는 LTA에 대해 기
존 5년에서 추가 연장에 대한 논의가 진행 중이며, 2029년까지 HBM, DRAM은 사실
상 완판, HBM 수요는 공급 가능 물량의 2 배를 상회, 2030 년까지 메모리 수요 우위 
국면 전망을 밝혔다. AI 인프라 투자의 강력한 명분과 메모리 수요의 구조성, 잠재적으
로 매우 높은 LTA 위반 비용을 감안하면 실적 가시성과 안정성 제고가 동반되는 국면
이다. 100 조원 수준의 주주환원은 2026 년 예상 FCF 의 66% 수준, 분기별 자사주 
매입, 소각 지속 계획을 통해 높은 주주환원 의지를 보여주고 있다. 실적 Duration 장
기화, 주주환원을 감안하면 12MF P/E 3.4X 수준의 현 주가는 현저한 저평가 국면이
다. 과도한 우려의 되돌림과 메모리 밸류에 대한 재논의 국면 진입을 전망한다.

### Page 1

삼성바이오로직스 (207940/KS) 
3Q26 Pre: 파업과 환율 부담에도 컨센 부합 
2026-10-06

제약/바이오. 이선경 / seonkyoung.lee@sks.co.kr / 3773-9089

매수(유지) 
목표주가: 2,000,000 원(유지) 
현재주가: 1,354,000 원 
상승여력: 47.7%

Signal: 파업과 환율 부담에도 컨센 부합 전망  
Key: 5 공장 매출 본격화, 미국 공장 매출 기여에 따른 성장 기대   
Step: 단기 불확실성보다는 중장기적 성장 방향성에 주목

3Q26 Pre: 파업과 환율 부담에도 성장지속, 컨센 부합 전망   
26년 3분기 연결기준 매출액은 1조 3,741억원(+9.3%YoY, +4.0%QoQ), 영업이
익은 6,098억원(-3.7%YoY, +4.0%QoQ, OPM 44.4%)으로 컨센서스 매출액 1
조 3,450억원과 영업이익 6,063억원에 부합하는 실적을 전망한다.  
5월 파업 영향으로 약 20 개 배치 생산분(약 1,500억원)에 대한 원가 손실이 인식되
고 3분기 평균 환율이 전분기 대비 약 5% 하락했음에도 불구하고, 3분기부터 매출로 
인식되는 5공장 PPQ 배치 및 미국 공장의 매출 기여로 지속적인 성장이 예상된다. 약 
1,500 억원 규모의 생산 차질분은 고객사와 일정 조율을 통해 출하될 예정으로 이에 
따른 4분기 매출 성장을 기대해 볼 수 있다.   
신규 수주 회복과 모달리티 확장에 주목 
시장에서 우려했던 신규 수주는 관세 이슈로 고객사의 컨택이 감소하면서 올해 저조했
으나 4 월 Section 232 결과 발표 이후 논의가 회복 단계에 있어 연내 추가 신규 수주 
가능성은 열려 있다.  
항체 시장의 경쟁심화와 차세대 모달리티 확장에 대응하기 위해 결정한 폴리펩타이드
의 인수는 연내 마무리될 예정이다. 폴리펩타이드의 2028 년 목표 EBITDA 마진은 
25%로 동사 대비 낮은 상황이나, 펩타이드 기반 CDMO 시장 성장에 따른 마진 개선
도 가능하다. 현재 임단협, 환율, 미국 공장 및 폴리펩타이드 인수에 따른 회계 영향 등 
불확실성이 큰 상황이나, 수주 회복과 5 공장 매출 인식 본격화, 미국 공장 및 멀티 모
달리티 확장 등 중장기 성장 방향성에 주목할 필요가 있다.

TUẦN 26-30/10 [Điểm nhấn thị trường] 
VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -32.53 điểm tương ứng -1.83%, với thanh 
khoản suy giảm

[Nhận định xu hướng] 
Mặc dù rủi ro mở rộng thêm nhịp điều chỉnh vẫn còn để ngỏ, tuy nhiên khi xu 
hướng tăng ngắn hạn đang được bảo lưu, VNINDEX được kỳ vọng sẽ cho phản 
ứng hồi phục trở lại tại các điểm đỡ bên dưới

### Page 2

2

Điểm nhấn thị trường 
Chỉ số, nhóm ngành, khối ngoại và phái sinh

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -32.53 điểm tương ứng -1.83%, với thanh khoản suy 
giảm. Linh kiện ô tô, Dầu khí và Công nghệ là các nhóm ngành có mức giảm rõ rệt nhất 
trong tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị -634 tỷ 
đồng, tập trung ở các mã FPT, ACB và VPL; mặt khác, tự doanh đảo chiều bán ròng ở mức 
-609 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại đảo chiều bán ròng -5,829 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần 
qua và đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -1,462 hợp đồng. Tự doanh duy trì mua ròng 722 hợp 
đồng nhưng đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -5,278 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam đánh mất đà hồi phục của tuần trước, khi VN-Index quay đầu giảm khoảng 
1,8% về đóng cửa quanh 1.742 điểm, với phiên cuối tuần có thời điểm lùi sâu về sát 1.727 
điểm trước khi thu hẹp đà giảm. Dù mùa báo cáo KQKD quý 3 diễn ra tương đối tích cực, 
tuy nhiên các lo ngại về việc môi trường lãi suất cao vẫn duy trì trong bối cảnh căng thẳng 
Trung Đông leo thang đang đè nặng lên tâm lý nhà đầu tư. Kết quả kỳ họp của FED trong 
tuần qua nhìn chung phù hợp với kỳ vọng và không gây ra ảnh hưởng đáng kể. Điểm kém 
tích cực nhất là khối ngoại đã quay lại bán ròng với tổng giá trị khoảng 2.700 tỷ đồng 
trong tuần — đảo ngược diễn biến mua ròng cuối tuần trước, cho thấy đà rút vốn ngoại 
vẫn chưa thực sự dừng lại.

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX tuần qua hình thành mẫu nến giảm trên đồ thị tuần, nhưng chưa thoái lui quá 1 
nửa cây nến tăng của tuần trước, cùng thanh khoản được tiết giảm, hàm ý nhịp điều chỉnh 
vẫn trong phạm vi cho phép. Trên khung ngày, sau bốn phiên giằng co đầu tuần với thanh 
khoản thấp, chỉ số đã chịu áp lực sụt giảm mạnh hơn trong phiên cuối tuần, chớm xuống 
dưới đường tín hiệu chính giữa dải Bollinger, nhưng cho tín hiệu rút chân ngay sau đó, thể 
hiện áp lực bán tháo đã tạm thời được kiểm soát. Mặc dù rủi ro mở rộng thêm nhịp điều 
chỉnh vẫn còn để ngỏ, tuy nhiên khi xu hướng tăng ngắn hạn đang được bảo lưu, VNINDEX 
được kỳ vọng sẽ cho phản ứng hồi phục trở lại tại các điểm đỡ bên dưới.

(Ghi chú: NĐT có thể dựa theo nhận định xu hướng thị trường để tham khảo khuyến nghị 
hành động tương ứng trong bảng phía trên) 
Kháng cự xa: 1850 (+-15) 
Kháng cự gần: 1800 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ gần: 1720 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ xa: 1680 (+-15)

### Page 4

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

Phương pháp tiếp cận: 
- Danh mục tập hợp các cổ phiếu tiềm năng nhất được KBSV 
lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng. 
- Ngưỡng cắt lỗ cần được cân nhắc cho mỗi mã là -15%. 
- Danh mục giả định luôn duy trì tỷ trọng 100% là cổ phiếu với 
trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

Lưu ý:  
- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua. 
- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua. 
 So sánh hiệu suất với VN30 Index

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu 
Mã Giá đóng cửa tuần Biến động từ đầu tháng Giá khuyến nghị mua Giá mục tiêu Lợi nhuận  kỳ vọng Điểm nhấn đầu tư Báo cáo phân tích 
FRT 148.70 1.2% 143.40 165.00 15.1% 
- Sau khi phủ sóng các thành phố lớn, chuỗi Long Châu đang mở rộng sang các vùng cấp 2, 3. 
Năm 2026 dự kiến mở thêm 400-450 cửa hàng (+20%YoY) với DT trên mỗi cửa hàng cũ tăng 
trưởng 6%-8%/năm 
- Dư địa lấy thêm thị phần từ các cửa hàng nhỏ lẻ vẫn còn tương đối rộng, FRT đặt mục tiêu 
chiếm 50% thị phần doanh thu ngành bán lẻ dược phẩm, từ mức 17%-20% hiện tại 
- Tiềm năng dài hạn từ mảng tiêm chủng tương đối tích cực

Document 301: CPI：10月环比双超预期，核心通胀连续三月黏在0.3%

Source PDF: all report pdf/CPI：10月环比双超预期，核心通胀连续三月黏在0.3%.pdf
Report source: 浙商期货
Date: 2026-11-12
Pages: 6
Status: ok

### Page 1

CPI ： 10 月环比双超预期，核心通胀连续三月黏在 0.3%
期货研究报告
发布时间： 2026 年 11 月 10 日
适用周期： 2026 年 11 月 10 日 —2026 年 12 月 10 日
浙商期货有限公司　期货交易咨询业务资格：证监许可 [2011]1287 号
【撰写人】浙商期货有限公司  郑弘 (Z0015227)
【咨询电话】 0571-87215357
核心结论： 10 月 CPI 环比 +0.4% 、核心 CPI 环比 +0.3% ，双双高于预期，核心通胀连续三个月停在 0.3% 附近，未给出降温证据。住房环比 +0.3% 贡献整体涨幅三成以上，食品
由持平转为 +0.5% ，能源涨幅由 9 月的 +5.2% 收窄至 +0.7% ，是唯一明确的缓和项。由于 2025 年 10 月政府停摆导致基期数据缺失， BLS 本月未公布同比，市场定价完全转向
环比读数。数据不支持 12 月暂停加息， 25bp 为基准情形、 50bp 进入视野；同比口径要等 12 月 10 日才能恢复。

看点一｜总量环比双超预期： CPI 环比 +0.4% （预期 +0.3% ，前值 +0.6% ），核心 CPI 环比 +0.3% （预期 +0.2% ，前值 +0.3% ），季调 CPI 指数由 336.30 升至 337.48 。
看点二｜主矛盾回到住房与食品：住房环比 +0.3% 、占月度涨幅三成以上，业主等价租金 +0.3% 、租金 +0.2% ；食品由持平转为 +0.5% ，杂货与外出就餐同涨。
看点三｜能源是唯一缓和项：能源环比 +0.7% （ 9 月 +5.2% ），汽油 +1.0% （ 9 月 +9.1% ），电费 +0.9% ，天然气价格下跌。
看点四｜政策性含义清晰：这是 12 月 8-9 日议息会议前最后一份 CPI ，市场已完全消化加息 25bp 并加大对 50bp 的押注。
一、总体数据一览

10 月同比缺位是本月最重要的技术事实： BLS 明确表示， 2025 年 10 月政府停摆期间未能采集价格数据且无法追溯补采，基期缺失导致 12 个月同比无法计算，实际收入同比同样不发布。因此本月不能沿
用 “ 同比为主线 ” 的常规框架，环比与三个月年化成为唯一可比的定价依据。
二、分项深度拆解
2.1 能源：涨幅大幅收窄，但未转为负贡献

能源环比由 9 月的 +5.2% 降至 +0.7% ，其中汽油由 +9.1% 降至 +1.0% ，电费 +0.9% ，天然气价格下跌形成部分抵消。同比端能源仍在 20.4% 的高位（ 9 月，数据库口径），对整体 CPI 的推升未消退，只是边

际减弱。结论是能源从 “ 加速器 ” 降为 “ 稳定器 ” ，尚不构成下拉力量。

没有更超预期的主要缓冲；关税对商品端的第二轮传导尚未在读数中显现。

住房环比 +0.3% ，对月度整体涨幅贡献三成以上，业主等价租金 +0.3% 、租金 +0.2% ，住房黏性未破。运输服务项分化最明显：交通运输服务环比 +0.8% 、机动车保险 +0.7% 、机票 +0.4% ，同比已升至

三、 CPI 分项趋势图表
三张图均截至 2026-09 ： 10 月同比因基期缺失未公布，无法落在同比序列上，是本月图表的技术限制。 10 月读数的边际变化请以第一节环比表与第二节分项环比为准。

线衔接、未插值。
四、市场反应与货币政策
📊  利率预期定价：数据公布前，利率期货已高度定价 12 月加息；公布后，市场完全消化 12 月加息 25 个基点，并明显加大对加息 50 个基点的押注，同时对 2027 年年中前至少再加息两次的定价上升。数
据走强而非走弱，是定价方向没有再摇摆的直接原因。按公司数据著录规则，此处概率仅作方向表述，不引用具体数值。
🧭  官员表态：理事沃勒在数据公布前已明确支持 12 月加息，并设置了一个可验证的门槛 —— 若核心 CPI 环比在 0.3% 或更高，应考虑加息 50 个基点；若通胀改善，则支持 25 个基点。 10 月核心 CPI 环比

+0.3% 恰好落在其 50bp 门槛上，这是本月数据对政策路径最直接的影响。
关键错位：数据公布前，市场共识曾预期核心 CPI 环比仅 +0.2% （部分机构更低），主要依据是健康保险统计方法重置与二手车价格回落等 “ 软因素 ” 。实际 +0.3% 说明这些技术性下拉未能压过住
房与食品的推动。这也意味着，即使后续出现单月走软，只要住房不降，核心通胀的粘性判断难以改变。
五、展望与风险
5.1 情景推演
通胀温和回落
40%
11 月环比回到 0.2% 附近，住房与运输服务同步降温，核心通胀折年率
向 2% 靠拢。
触发条件：住房环比降至 0.2% 以下、能源转负、油价维持 100 美元下方。
能源反复
35%
中东谈判反复推升油价，能源重新成为月度涨幅主力，核心商品跟随
成本端抬升。
触发条件：霍尔木兹通航未实质恢复、布伦特重回 110 美元上方。
滞胀风险
25%
通胀连续三月 0.3% 叠加消费放缓，政策被迫更紧，增长与通胀反向恶
化。
触发条件：核心 CPI 连续第四月 ≥0.3% 、零售与消费者信心同步走弱。

### Page 6

当前最核心的不确定因素：能源向核心的二次传导。能源价格同比仍在 20% 以上，对燃料敏感行业已开始转嫁成本；若传导在四季度扩散，核心服务与核心商品的降温节奏会被推后， 50bp 加息的概率随
之上升。另一个不可忽视的变量是统计口径本身 —— 同比在 12 月 10 日恢复后，市场会重新得到一个 “ 可读的 ” 通胀水平，届时的重定价可能比单月环比更剧烈。
5.2 关键风险矩阵
🔴  油价反弹风险
中东局势若再度升级，能源将从稳定器回到推手，能源同比在 20% 以上的基数上继续抬升，直接压低核心
通胀回落的可信度。
🔴  关税第二轮冲击
商品端目前尚有二手车与通信服务对冲，一旦关税成本在服装、家电等品类集中体现，核心商品将转为正
贡献，核心 CPI 难以下破 0.3% 。
六、结论
1. 总量判断：10 月 CPI 环比 +0.4% 、核心环比 +0.3% ，双双高于预期，价格压力在中期选举前仍在累积，未出现实质性降温证据。
2. 能源主因转换：能源环比由 +5.2% 收窄至 +0.7% ，是本月唯一明确的缓和项；但同比仍在 20.4% 高位，仅为边际减弱，不构成下拉。

3. 核心通胀分化：住房环比 +0.3% 贡献整体涨幅三成以上、交通运输服务与车险走强，医疗保健同比降至 1.5% 、信息技术与服装同比回落，核心服务强、核心商品弱。
4. 政策路径：市场完全消化 12 月加息 25bp 并加大 50bp 押注，核心 CPI 连续三月 0.3% 恰好触及沃勒提出的 50bp 门槛； 25bp 为基准情形， 50bp 为上行风险。
5. 不确定性警示：本月无同比读数，单月环比不足以宣告趋势；真正的验证点在 12 月 10 日同比恢复后的 11 月数据，以及能源向核心的传导是否扩散。
数据说明：① 10 月 CPI 与核心 CPI 的 12 个月同比未公布，原因为 2025 年 10 月政府停摆期间 BLS 未采集价格数据且无法追溯补采，基期缺失；实际收入同比同样未公布。② 10 月环比与分项环比取自 BLS 官方数据表，投研数据库尚未更新 10 月
值。③同比历史序列取自浙商投研数据库，最新更新至 2026-09 。④利率期货概率为媒体引用值，按公司数据著录规则仅作方向表述。⑤报告中不含债券收益率、汇率、贵金属等资产价格明细。

务必向专业人士咨询并谨慎决策。

Document 316: Europe’s Deal Limiting Chinese Auto Imports (2026-11-02)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Europe’s Deal Limiting Chinese Auto Imports (2026-11-02).pdf
Report source: CFR (Follow the Money)
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 16
Status: ok

### Page 1

1
EXPERT TAKE FOLLOW THE MONEY
Europe’s Deal Limiting Chinese Auto Imports
Europe’s auto deal with China is a good start. But containing China’s export boom
will require a much broader response.
European Commissioner for Trade and Economic Security Maros Sefcovic speaks at a press briefing at the EU Delegation to
China in Beijing, China October 9, 2026. Florence Lo/Reuters
PUBLISHED
Articles Follow the Money Share
Log In
Regions Topics Research Experts Events The Future of American Strategy

### Page 2

2
November 2, 2026 11:02 a.m.
Brad W. Setser CFR EXPERT
Whitney Shepardson Senior Fellow
The year-over-year increase in Chinese automobile
exports to all markets is currently about 3.5 million
cars.
Over ten years, that is 35 million cars. The global
market excluding China is only around 60 million
cars. Japan raised its auto exports by about 3.5 million
cars in the 1970s—but that was over a decade, not
one year. And right now the U.S. and Indian markets

(around 20 million cars) are effectively closed to
Chinese production. The 2026 jump in exports is an
incredible.
China’s domestic market, though, is down 20 percent
this year—that works out to a decline of about 5
million cars. The Wall Street Journal estimates that
domestic sales will fall to around 19 million cars this
year, less than a third of the production capacity of
China’s auto industry.
So even though China’s exports are increasing at an

unprecedented pace, China’s domestic auto sector
isn’t really booming; production is essentially flat, and
intense domestic competition is destroying the
profitability of firms that don’t export.*

### Page 3

3
Europe’s new auto deal with China should be
understood in this context.
The deal limits China’s exports of hybrids (including
plug-in hybrids) to Europe to around 350,000 cars.
That is above the 300,000 hybrids that China
exported to Europe in 2025, but well below the (gulp)
800,000 annualized pace for hybrids in the last three
months of (European) data. Total imports,
annualized, were running at an incredible 2 million a
year pace thanks to strong battery electric imports in

September.

### Page 4

4
China appears to have concluded that it prefers a
guaranteed market for a limited number of cars (and
more or less guaranteed profits) to the risk of a
prohibitive tariff that would cut off all its exports.
And Europe preferred a negotiated deal to the risk of
retaliation and a slowdown in licensing rare earth and
magnet exports.
The Commission also thinks of this as a bit of a
model—more on that later.
I certainly understand the logic of the deal.

Something needed to be done. China’s export of
hybrids to Europe were up an insane 200 percent
year-over-year in the last 3 months of data, which led
to a 75 percent increase for all cars. One more year of
75 percent growth would bring China’s exports to

### Page 5

5
around 3 million cars; in two years, close to 6 million,
and in three years, 10 million—or most of the market.
But Europe still is a long way from a coherent trade
and industrial policy with respect to autos, let alone a
coherent economic policy towards overall trade with
China.
After this deal, as I understand it:
Hybrid imports are capped at 350,000, down
from a projected 800,000 in 2026—and then
rising to 370,000 and 390,000 in subsequent
years.

Battery electric vehicles face a tariff from 25
percent (Telsa, 10 percent base + 15 percent) to
45 percent (10 percent base plus 35 percent).
BYD’s total tariff is at 27 percent, which isn’t
prohibitive. Even with these tariffs, battery
electric imports will total around 600,000 this
year, up from 500,000 last year.
Diesel and gasoline (ICE) cars face a tariff of
10 percent. Europe is importing around
200,000 of these a year (including a significant

number from Chinese-owned MG).
There are no special tariffs on imported parts or
imported batteries, which matters for the growing
number of Chinese factories in Europe. So long as
tariffs are low and the renminbi is undervalued,
Chinese firms still have an incentive to maximize the

### Page 6

6
Chinese content of their “transplant” factories (ask
Thailand).
There is currently no unified European policy on
inward investment that requires joint ventures or
technology transfer either, only a lot of wishful
thinking.**
Sum it up, and it is a market where Chinese exports
are constrained by a hodge-podge of different trade
defense tools, and there is no standard policy for
ensuring that Chinese assembly operations use
European parts rather than import Chinese kits. The

existing operations, which are just starting to scale up,
don’t draw on a European supply chain. They likely
preserve a few assembly jobs at the expense of the
broader automotive ecosystem.
A coherent European (or national) industrial policy
for the automotive and battery sectors is thus still a
work in progress.
The safeguard on hybrids and the resulting tariff rate
quota only stops the bleeding.
And make no mistake, Europe’s position in the global

automotive sector is slipping. Europe still runs an
overall surplus in auto trade, but that surplus is falling
fast if trade in batteries is added to automotive trade
(there isn’t a split between batteries used for energy

### Page 7

7
storage and batteries used by cars in the EU data
right now, at least not that I have found).
Europe’s broader trade policy to China is similarly a
work in progress.
The surge in imports of hybrid cars was particularly
pronounced, but it is by no means the only such surge.
The balance in industrial machinery has eroded
significantly. Consider the following chart, which
shows imports and exports of machinery, electronics
and vehicles (HS 84, 85, and 87) by weight.

### Page 8

8
The balance in chemicals, measured by weight to
abstract from price changes, shows the same trend.
Europe doesn’t have an across-the-board tool for
applying tariffs against China like the U.S. section
301 statute.

### Page 9

9
But that isn’t necessarily an immediate constraint, as
there are plenty of solid safeguard cases thanks to the
current surge in European imports from China.
But if each safeguard needs to include a negotiated
tariff-rate quota that gives China enough guaranteed
market access (and Chinese firms enough guaranteed
profits) to ensure trade peace, Europe needs to get
moving with negotiation in other sectors.
Chemicals and machinery are more diverse sectors

than cars and thus aren’t as amenable to the same
high-level Š ef č ovi č -Wang negotiations the led to the
most recent deal.
This critique isn’t meant as criticism. Europe needed
to act on cars. Look at a chart of automotive trade in
kilos.

### Page 10

10
It’s just that it also needs to act in other sectors: auto
parts, batteries (though that is complicated),
chemicals, and some categories of industrial
machinery. The scale of China’s export boom and the
fact that in most sectors there are no limits to China’s
ability to scale up is a real problem.***
And Europe cannot wait for China to conclude that
it would rather grow on the basis of internal, rather
than external, demand.
I disagree with the IMF’s former top brass on this. A

China that grows on the back of a 1.5 pp net export
contribution for another couple of years will
deindustrialize much of the world. China’s trading
partners cannot step aside and do nothing until
China figures out how to escape deflation using its
own demand. Xi appears more than happy to
continue with his current policy mix and gamble that

### Page 11

11
the world will continue to absorb China’s ever
growing export surplus.
Pascal Lamy, Nicolas Köhler-Suzuki, and Victor Luiz
do Prado of the Jacques Delors Institute recognize
the need for a more systemic response—as do Jean-
Pierre Landau and Sébastien Jean, who have done an
excellent job explaining how China’s micro- and
macro-industrial policies fit together into a coherent,
self-reinforcing whole.
The proposals in the Lamy et al. paper overlap heavily

with the Commission’s current approach to China.
The authors envision a two-pronged approach: first,
negotiating with China to seek agreement on tariff-
rate quotas that effectively limit the EU’s imports
from China; and second, developing a broad yet
targeted tariff-based instrument to address structural
imbalances should negotiations fail. The latter would
give Europe a credible threat should China refuse to
agree to the proposed safeguards.
My own view: don’t let the perfect be the enemy of

the good.
The EU has shied away from developing its own 301
tool out of concern about preserving a “rules-based
system.” I still think a European 301 would allow a
more fulsome response, but understand that
European isn’t as willing as the United States to use

### Page 12

12
tools that at least appear to be outside those
authorized by the WTO. In reality the EU no longer
really wants to trade with China on the same (MFN)
terms applied to other WTO members. That mirrors
the current view of the United States. But the EU is
willing to go through more procedural hoops to get to
a similar result.****
But I also recognize that, absent policy adjustment in
China—notably a stronger currency that allows for
fair market competition—the scope of safeguards and

managed trade will progressively expand.
* One sign: Stellantis is resuming Jeep production in
China, but primarily for export rather than the
domestic market.
** There is a lot of (in my view) somewhat confused
analysis of Chinese investment that works off the
analogy provided by either foreign investment in
China’s auto sector or Japanese investment in the U.S.
and Europe in the 1980s. These are relevant examples
of course, but asserting that Chinese investment will

result in a similar outcome when the underlying
economics differ is a big logical jump. Japanese firms
had incentives to localize their parts production in the
United States from the then-strong yen as well as
from the risk of protection. Foreign firms operating in
China had to do JVs until recently and had incentives
to localize from China’s significant tariff on auto

### Page 13

13
parts and China’s lower cost structure (in part because
of a weak RMB). Tesla’s Shanghai incentives and tax
deal included strong localization incentives, and there
were additional incentives from Tesla’s desire to
qualify for the lists the governed access to China’s
consumer EV subsidies. That is why something like
90 percent of Tesla parts, including its batteries, are
now made in China. Chinese investors in Europe
have no similar incentives to localize right now, yet

many assume that the results will be the same. That,
to me, is naïve. Since the underlying economics differ
(Europe’s cost basis is much higher than existing
production in China) getting Chinese firms to do
more than final assembly in Europe will take stronger
policy tools than were required to get foreign firms to
localize parts production in China. Europe’s
Industrial Accelerator Act would be a step in the
right direction, but more is likely needed. Thailand

provides a useful cautionary example.
*** China can make over 60 million cars, according to
recent reporting from the Wall Street Journal. The
domestic market this year will be under 20 million
cars (down from something like 24 million cars last
year). Even with exports of 10 million, there is ~30
million cars worth of unused auto production
capacity (roughly double European demand). And
China is still investing at a pace that is consistent
with adding close to 5 million of EV production

capacity a year. Chinese firms, who don’t make any
money on domestic sales, are strongly incentivized to

### Page 14

14
This work represents the views solely of the author(s). The

nonpartisan membership organization, think tank, and
publisher, and takes no institutional positions on matters
of policy.
From Follow the Money and Greenberg Center for Geoeconomics
export. The world has never encountered such elastic
supply.
**** The more different the legal basis for the
European and U.S. response—and the more policy
differences (TRQs vs. a flat tariff, for example) the

harder it would be to ever develop a harmonized
policy approach. But with President Trump, a
harmonized policy isn’t even a U.S. goal.

Document 330: Global Views_ Downside Growth Risks

Source PDF: all report pdf/Global Views_ Downside Growth Risks.pdf
Report source: Goldman Sachs Publishing
Date: 2026-10-26
Pages: 10
Status: ok

### Page 1

Jan Hatzius 
+1(212)902-0394 | jan.hatzius@gs.com 
Goldman Sachs & Co. LLC
Global Views: Downside Growth Risks
26 October 2026 | 6:45AM EDT
1. So far in 2026, global growth has been remarkably unremarkable.  Both the world 
as a whole and the G3 economies are growing in line with our estimates of potential 
GDP.  Our interpretation is that fiscal easing and the positive impulse from the AI 
boom have offset the negative impulse from the war in Iran and the lingering drag 
from higher US tariffs.

2. Our standing global GDP forecasts project more of the same in 2027, as lower 
energy prices offset fading tailwinds from fiscal policy and AI.  But the balance of 
risks is on the downside.  The most immediate concern is that, far from subsiding, 
energy prices have again climbed sharply on fears of renewed Middle East escalation.  
The prices of gasoline, diesel, jet fuel, and European/Asian natural gas are now near 
or even above their March/April highs, with likely negative effects on consumers and 
energy-importing economies more broadly.

### Page 2

3. Although we don’t expect significant fiscal policy changes in 2027, the consequences 
of large-scale deficits—coupled with AI-linked borrowing and central bank hikes—have 
started to show up more clearly in higher longer-term interest rates.  The moves have 
been mostly orderly, but the increase since early 2026 averages a sizable 100bp across 
DM yield curves.  If sustained, we estimate that this increase will subtract a cumulative 
0.6% from GDP via weakness in housing, consumer durables, and business investment.  
(Only part of the drag is incorporated in our standing forecasts because we expect bond 
yields to pull back as central banks deliver fewer hikes than priced in markets.)

Exhibit 2: Oil Price Risks Are Skewed to the Upside

Source: Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research
26 October 2026   2
Goldman Sachs Global Views

### Page 3

4. The growth impulse from the AI boom was set to peak in late 2026 or early 2027 even 
before concerns around the sustainability of the AI investment boom intensified in 
recent weeks.  Although AI investment is still rising rapidly, we expect the increase as a 
share of GDP to slow over the next year.  Alongside the fading boost from last year’s 
increase in US depreciation allowances, this will weigh on capital spending growth, 
especially in the US.  AI has also boosted US consumer spending growth via a positive 
wealth effect worth an estimated 0.4pp (or 0.25% of GDP) over the past year.  This 
impact is likely to decline over the next year, gradually if stocks perform in line with our 
strategists’ reasonably upbeat expectations but more sharply if AI-related stocks 
continue to decline.

### Page 4

5. Our own base case remains that AI investment will ultimately pay off in terms of higher 
productivity and economywide profits.  However, one key channel for this payoff is labor 
market disruption, which has negative near-term macroeconomic effects.  And while DM 
labor markets still look solid in aggregate, we are starting to see clearer signs that the AI 
impact is spreading beyond the technology sector.  Across nonmanufacturing industries, 
the share of US firms that have adopted AI in regular business functions now shows a 
statistically significant negative correlation with employment growth and a statistically 
significant positive correlation with unemployment.

6. The Fed’s recent signals—explicit from Williams/Jefferson and implicit from Warsh—
effectively rule out a hike on October 28.  But the FOMC still looks very likely to deliver 
another 25bp hike to 4-4.25% on December 9 because spot inflation remains too high.  
And while we continue to expect significant disinflation next year on the back of 
reduced impulses from tariffs, energy prices, and AI-related prices, the risks to our 
forecast are on the upside, most obviously in energy.  Higher inflation could result in

another hike in March, but either way we see renewed cuts in late 2027/early 2028.

7. Like the Fed, the ECB will likely hold rates steady next week and deliver a final hike (to 
2.75%) at the December meeting.  However, the turbulence in the French bond market 
poses somewhat greater downside risks to that call than in the US.  The turbulence has 
tightened financial conditions to a degree that is likely to shave ¼-½pp from annualized 
growth in coming quarters.  And if it intensifies into December, markets might perceive a 
hike as signaling insufficient focus by the ECB on the risk of Euro area fragmentation.  
While the ECB could address this issue by coupling a hike with renewed balance sheet 
expansion, the hurdle for such a move is high, in part because purchases of French 
bonds would inevitably raise concerns about moral hazard with respect to the needed 
fiscal adjustment.

8. Across the English Channel, we expect the MPC to raise Bank Rate by 25bp to 4% on

### Page 6

November 5.  Consistent with our view that the UK economy is stronger than widely 
perceived, GDP growth has surprised on the upside this year and the unemployment rate 
declined to 4.7% in August on a 3-month average basis (with the volatile 1-month rate 
plunging to 4.1%).  Rising inflation and the looming household energy price reset in 
January imply a significant risk of further moves, although market pricing of 4+ hikes 
(including November) still looks excessive.  We also expect hikes in Canada and New 
Zealand this week and in Australia next week.

9. Slightly faster than expected growth in Q3 has pushed up our 2026 GDP forecast for 
China to 4.6%.  But most activity indicators are much weaker.  Both property activity and 
fixed asset investment are contracting year-on-year, and while nominal retail sales 
growth picked up to +1.1% in September, large retailers (whose sales are relatively 
better measured) are still posting substantial year-on-year declines.  With domestic 
demand rising slowly at best, sustained GDP growth in the official 4.5-5% target range 
will require ongoing steep increases in the trade surplus.  But the surplus is already at 1% 
of global GDP, and further increases will eat more noticeably into output and 
employment abroad.  Ultimately, China’s trading partners may respond by imposing 
trade barriers that weigh on Chinese exports and GDP, although the measures taken so 
far have been limited.

Exhibit 8: UK Growth Forecasts Have Drifted Higher

Source: Bloomberg, Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research
26 October 2026   6
Goldman Sachs Global Views

### Page 7

10. Our strongest global view is that central banks will deliver fewer hikes than currently 
priced.  In addition, our rates strategists flag underweight positioning, diminished 
technical risks, and risk asset pressures as reasons why the recent rally might extend.  
The market’s narrow breadth makes our equity strategists wary of momentum factor 
exposure, but they still expect the S&P 500 to resume its climb after the midterm 
election, as earnings continue to grow solidly.  Our credit strategists remain cautious on 
IG technology bonds but see scope for some near-term reversal in HY given the scale of 
the recent selloff.  Our FX strategists expect the Japanese yen to act as a hedge against 
further AI jitters, in part because such jitters would shrink US-Japan interest rate 
differentials.  And our commodity strategists recommend targeted exposures to oil 
products and European natural gas to hedge near-term escalation risks alongside 
longer-term exposures to copper, gold, and select power markets to benefit from 
structural supply/demand imbalances. 
Jan Hatzius

Document 361: OpenAI解散机器人团队！GPT-6 Astra证明：通用大模型已可直接用于“物理AI”，无需专用模型

Source PDF: all report pdf/OpenAI解散机器人团队！GPT-6 Astra证明：通用大模型已可直接用于“物理AI”，无需专用模型.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-14
Pages: 4
Status: ok

### Page 1

OpenAI 称，公司研究⼈员原本认为，推进机器⼈技术的最佳⽅式是建⽴在 Sora 等模型的研
究成果之上，但此后发现 GPT-6 Astra 等通⽤前沿模型已经展现出直接应⽤于机器⼈领域的
能⼒，这促使公司解散独⽴机器⼈团队，将相关研究⼈员转移⾄更⼴泛的通⽤ AI 模型研发
团队。
摘要：
OpenAI 解散独⽴机器⼈团队，押注通⽤模型直驱具⾝智能。
11 ⽉ 13 ⽇，据《财富》杂志报道，OpenAI 已解散独⽴运营的机器⼈团队，将相关研究⼈员分拨
⾄其他部⻔。公司表⽰，此举的直接导⽕索是 GPT-6 Astra 等通⽤前沿模型的意外突破。
研究⼈员发现，这些模型可被直接应⽤于机器⼈控制任务，⽆需为机器⼈单独开发专⽤模型。
在英伟达的⼀项测试中，由 Astra 驱动的智能体在模拟操作基准测试中达到 74.8% 的成功率，优
于该榜单上最佳专⽤机器⼈模型报告的 51.0% 。
此次重组同样发⽣在 OpenAI 承压削减成本的背景之下。据报道，该公司最近⼀个季度的运营亏
损⾼达 109 亿美元，同时正寻求完成新⼀轮融资，并为 2027 年初可能的 IPO 做准备。

⾸席执⾏官 Sam Altman 此前已要求公司放弃 " 旁⽀任务 " ，将资源集中于核⼼产品。
团队去向：研究与应⽤双线并⼊
OpenAI 确认，机器⼈研究团队已并⼊公司主研究团队，向⾸席研究官 Mark Chen 汇报；应⽤机
器⼈与硬件团队则划归更⼤的 Applied 组织，向公司总裁 Greg Brockman 汇报。
这已是 OpenAI 在机器⼈领域的第⼆次收缩。该公司早期曾设有机器⼈团队，于 2020 年将其裁
撤。
联合创始⼈ Wojciech Zaremba 当时的理由是机器⼈训练数据匮乏，且通过其他路径，尤其是在
海量⼈类书写⽂本上训练超⼤型语⾔模型更能⾼效推进通⽤⼈⼯智能（ AGI ）的研发⽬标。
正是这条路径，最终在 2022 年底催⽣了 ChatGPT 。
OpenAI 于 2024 年重建独⽴机器⼈团队，宣布与⼈形机器⼈初创公司 Figure 合作，共同开发机器
⼈ AI 模型。该合作于 2025 年 2 ⽉告终， Figure 宣布将⾃主研发机器⼈ AI 。

OpenAI 表⽰⽬前仍在开发⾃研⼈形机器⼈， Altman 的⽬标是在 2027 年完成⾸次公开演⽰。
此次重组之仓促，使得 OpenAI 在解散团队后仍在本周于奥斯汀举办的顶级学术机器⼈会议
CoRL 上保留了展位注册。据《财富》杂志报道，该杂志记者赶赴 208 号展位时，发现展位空
置， OpenAI 的标识踪迹全⽆。
核⼼逻辑：通⽤模型使机器⼈不再是 " 特殊问题 "
OpenAI 在回应《财富》时阐明了这⼀战略转变背后的逻辑演变。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
该公司表⽰，年初时研究⼈员原本认为，推动机器⼈领域进展的最佳路径，是在 Sora 等视频⽣
成模型的⼯作基础上继续深耕。 Sora 为⽣成逼真视频，必须对物理世界的运⾏⽅式具备某种隐
性理解。
但此后， GPT-6 Astra 等通⽤前沿模型展现出可被直接应⽤于机器⼈控制的能⼒，这⼀发现促使
公司改变⽅向。
卡内基梅隆⼤学机器⼈研究所助理教授 Guanya Shi 将此次调整解读为⼀个信号： OpenAI 认为机
器⼈学 " 不再是⼀个特殊问题 " ，⽽是通⽤智能的⼀项下游应⽤。正如同⼀套底层前沿 AI 模型如今
已同时驱动代码、法律、⾦融等垂直领域的专业⼯具。
Shi 补充称，通⽤模型在空间感知和推理能⼒上的进步，已使其能够处理此前需要专⻔训练的任
务，例如应对瓶⼦摆放位置变化或光线条件改变，以及对任务步骤进⾏推理排序。

⾏业冲击：挑战机器⼈专⽤ AI 的底层逻辑
这⼀思路对当前机器⼈创业⽣态构成潜在挑战。众多机器⼈初创公司已在 " 机器⼈需要⾃有⼤
脑 " 的前提假设下募集了数⼗亿美元资⾦，⽽通⽤模型可直接⽤于机器⼈控制的可能性，从根本
上动摇了这⼀叙事。
不过， MIT 教授、 Walden Robotics 联合创始⼈兼 CEO Russ Tedrake 认为，机器⼈公司仍具备 AI
实验室不拥有的优势：它们的机器⼈已在⼯⼚⻋间等真实场所积累实际作业经验。这类 " 部署 " 数
据帮助机器⼈适应特定⼯作场景和任务。
Tedrake 指出，更强⼤的基础模型反⽽使这些场景专属数据更具价值，因为机器⼈可以从中更快
速地学习。
Generalist 联合创始⼈ Pete Florence 则持更为审慎的态度。他表⽰， " 培育智能的起点可能不⽌
⼀种 " ，⽬前尚不清楚哪个现有模型将最终主导机器⼈领域。
此外，技术层⾯的制约依然存在。 Shi 指出，⼤型语⾔模型虽然强⼤，但运⾏速度慢且依赖云端
部署，意味着机器⼈需要持续联⽹。他认为，能在机器⼈本地计算机上独⽴运⾏、⽆需持续⽹

络连接的⼩型专⽤模型仍有其存在价值。
成本压⼒：聚焦核⼼，剥离旁⽀
此次重组亦是 OpenAI 更⼤范围成本管控举措的组成部分。
Altman 去年已明确要求公司摒弃 " 旁⽀任务 " ，此后 OpenAI 已陆续削减若⼲项⽬，包括视频⽣成
模型 Sora ，以将算⼒资源重新调配⾄更具商业潜⼒的核⼼业务。
据报道， OpenAI 最近⼀个季度运营亏损达 109 亿美元。在筹备新融资轮次及谋划 2027 年初 IPO
的双重压⼒下，压缩亏损的迫切性持续上升。解散独⽴机器⼈团队、将相关⼈员并⼊主线研
究，既契合公司聚焦核⼼的战略导向，也有助于减少重复建设、提升研发效率。
OpenAI 表⽰，重新安置机器⼈研究⼈员⾄其他团队，是因为公司现在相信这是加速推进通⽤机
器⼈研发的最佳⽅式。公司强调，机器⼈仍是 OpenAI ⻓期愿景的核⼼组成部分。

Document 368: Sales Trader Commentary_ Squeezing the Shorts, Fading the Rallies; Navigating TAIEX Chaos Before Heading to the Vice City _ GSTW

Source PDF: all report pdf/Sales Trader Commentary_ Squeezing the Shorts, Fading the Rallies; Navigating TAIEX Chaos Before Heading to the Vice City _ GSTW.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-11
Pages: 11
Status: ok

### Page 1

Sales Trader Commentary: Squeezing the
Shorts, Fading the Rallies; Navigating TAIEX
Chaos Before Heading to the Vice City | GSTW
12 November 2026 | 3:15 PM Taiwan Standard Time
Robert Wang
Goldman Sachs (Asia) L.L.C. Taipei Branch
FICC & Equities
Post the historical open on 11/6/2026, we saw a jaw-dropping 14% climb from day-
low last Friday. Finally took a breather (and a big one too) today; admittedly, I was
writing a different narrative, but last hour fade in Taiwan has forced me to rewrite this

(>1k points drop and picked up market turnover). So, where do we go from here?
Check out below for my observation and investor feedback.
Key Investor Feedback and Takeaway
Sentiment. Sentiment is still cautious among foreigners; a lot of HFs faced serious
short squeeze from this rally. Yet, many are still reluctant to cover all their short
positions given the new short sell restrictions. and hence held on to the pain trades.
Interestingly, market still exhaust short sell quota almost every day. Additionally,

there is little conviction for a full-on rally and also low willingness to chase
around here given year-end. Overall, HFs have de-grossed much, one of the most
aggressive de-gross per GS prime book data. Key focus will be whether LOs are
willing to participate and to what extent will they add back the positions. Only
then, we will likely see a more sustainable rally further from here. Bottom line: are
we out of the woods? Not yet. Cautious optimism is perhaps the most positive

thing I can say for now.
Positioning. Foreigners have net sold for 10 straight sessions, totaling around
US$25.4bn. Most outflows are in TSMC but worth noting that we finally saw TSMC
net buy for the first time since 2 weeks yesterday. On the other hand, local funds
have been good size net buyers to absorb the foreign outflows, mainly from retail's
mutual fund subscription. Just regurgitating some of my berg blurbs from the past
few days, we still see lots of short interest in crowded long sectors, most in

ABF/CCL (i.e. NY Plastics) followed by CPO names; and most smid cap
outperformance was driven by short covering. The overly sold names saw little
attention and minimum desire to bottom fish (i.e. Delta, Chroma, KYEC). On a
brighter note, we also picked up some real money inflows into TSMC this week, but
I don't know whether it's enough to turn the tide. The more convicted long right
now for foreigners is still ASE in my opinion.
Retail. This is perhaps the most important factor now in Taiwan. Coupled with the

sentiment boost from National Stabilization Fund, many retail decided to hold
instead of cutting loss and even bought into the dip. For those retail that were
facing margin call during this correction, some were eventually saved by the market
rally. Cleaner positioning for sure, though perhaps not clean enough to fuel another
11/12/26, 3:20 PM Sales Trader Commentary: Squeezing the Shorts, Fading the Rallies; Navigating TAIEX Chaos Before Heading to the Vice City | GSTW

https://marquee.gs.com/s/content/markets/dc88e4dd-47de-45c1-bbf3-31c2c5c57299 1/11

### Page 2

10% climb in index. Per my local investor feedback, the smart pundits actually
many have missed out on this rally (of course they've also de-grossed much ahead).
Margin loan balance and maintenance ratio are two key data that I will follow
closely in the coming weeks.
Fundamentals. Fundamentals are still good with most company reporting record
high quarterly earnings. Monthly revenue also grew MoM. But stock trading is all
about forward looking. People are anticipating AI capex growth to slow down or

even plateau in the coming 2 years. Quanta (2382 TT) earnings call yesterday
validated this narrative - still high growth in 2027 but unlikely to see another triple-
digits rapid growth like 2026. Earnings outlooks probably matter less than
positioning and macro backdrop. With that, we will conclude most of our 3Q26
earnings this week with Hon Hai (2317 TT) as the main finale.
Liquidity Event: MSCI just published their quarterly review, and NYPCB (8046 TT)

and TUC (6274 TT) will be added into the index, while Lotes (3533 TT), Chailease
(5871 TT), Wan Hai (2615 TT), an E-Ink (8069 TT) will be removed. Kinsus (3189
TT) did not get added due to its disposition status. See below and this report for
our flows estimate.
Risk. Risk reward level here is not attractive, especially when the rebound came so
aggressively. Momentum to sustain the rally is not constructive, so investors should
be cautious of more volatility and further unwind (if there's anything to be

unwound). Jensen and Nvidia's headlines are certainly not helping, though I did see
some funny memes on the social media. Credit is another risk here; neoclouds top
line is +ve but let's not forget at what cost are these companies carving those
revenue from. Otherwise, market seems to process the macro prints well, with now
expecting a rate hike in Dec'26 and Mar'27. Last but not least, cross-strait tension
can be a focus next during Trump-Xi meeting at the APEC Summit next week. I

know the Taiwanese has been (probably will be) indifferent and just another BAU,
but anything assertively negative comment on Taiwan might fret the macro
traders, such as Xi's comment just now.
My View. Not a BEAR so far. I do not think the market is broken, but I
acknowledge the downside risk has risen again and enlarged as I respect the
technicals. That said, I am still excited about next week, even though we have fewer
fundamental catalysts now. In the US, Nvidia is set to report next week. A lighter

book might fare better in today's environment. To end on a fun personal note, GTA
VI will finally come out after 13 years of waiting. So if you can't find me on
Bloomberg, try looking for me in the Vice City.

Document 372: SpaceX与特斯拉世纪合并谈判提速，最快12月宣布交易

Source PDF: all report pdf/SpaceX与特斯拉世纪合并谈判提速，最快12月宣布交易.pdf

### Page 1

特斯拉定于 12 ⽉ 3 ⽇召开年度股东⼤会，这⼀时间节点与最快 12 ⽉宣布交易的时间线⾼度
重合。⽬前，双⽅正在讨论的核⼼议题之⼀，是 SpaceX 在交易中需向特斯拉投资者发⾏的
股份数量。与此同时，双⽅也讨论了公告推迟⾄ 2027 年初的备选⽅案。
摘要：
SpaceX 与特斯拉的合并谈判正在加速推进，这笔交易⼀旦落地，将成为全球有史以来规模最⼤
的并购案，彻底重塑全球交易市场的边界。
据彭博报道，知情⼈⼠透露，双⽅最快可能在 12 ⽉宣布这笔交易，这⼀激进时间表折射出⻢斯
克在企业运作上⼀贯的快节奏⻛格。与此同时，双⽅也讨论了公告推迟⾄ 2027 年初的备选⽅
案。消息传出后，特斯拉股价当⽇盘中最⾼涨幅达 1.9% ， SpaceX 股价同期上涨 0.5% 。
两家公司的董事会及顾问⽬前正就合并条款、合并后实体的治理架构等核⼼议题展开磋商。然
⽽，交易在正式落地前仍⾯临多重障碍，包括如何隔离特斯拉在中国的业务，以及 SpaceX 涉及
美国政府和国防合同所引发的国家安全敏感性问题。

谈判进展与时间节点
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
据彭博此前报道， SpaceX 与特斯拉此前的⽬标是在明年上半年宣布交易，并⼒争在特朗普总统
任期结束前完成合并。
⻢斯克于 10 ⽉披露，两家公司的董事会已分别成⽴由独⽴董事组成的特别委员会，专⻔负责评
估合并事宜。⽬前，双⽅正在讨论的核⼼议题之⼀，是 SpaceX 在交易中需向特斯拉投资者发⾏
的股份数量。
值得关注的是，特斯拉定于 12 ⽉ 3 ⽇召开年度股东⼤会，这⼀时间节点与最快 12 ⽉宣布交易的时
间线⾼度重合。
两家公司此前已存在业务协同基础 —— 双⽅正联合在德克萨斯州建设⼀座名为 "Terafab" 的半导
体⼯⼚，并持续推进联合运营的前期布局。
监管与政治⻛险构成关键障碍
尽管谈判提速，交易⾯临的障碍不容忽视。据报道，知情⼈⼠指出，如何隔离特斯拉在中国的
业务，是双⽅必须解决的核⼼难题之⼀。

摩根⼤通分析师 Rajat Gupta 上⽉在研报中向客⼾指出， SpaceX 涉及美国政府及国防合同的国家
安全敏感性，与特斯拉在中国规模庞⼤且盈利丰厚的制造业务，构成 " 相互对⽴的关键动态 " ，将
对交易获得监管及股东批准形成重⼤挑战。
与此同时，此次合并也将成为美国中期选举后政治⽣态的重要试⾦⽯。⺠主党在此次中期选举
中重新掌控国会，并已承诺对⻢斯克的商业及政治活动展开调查，这为交易的推进增添了额外
的政治不确定性。
交易规模史⽆前例， AI 战略逻辑驱动合并
此次合并将在华尔街掀起轩然⼤波。合并后的实体将囊括电动汽⻋、储能电池、⽕箭及卫星业
务，合并体量按当前市值估算超过 3.4 万亿美元——SpaceX 市值逾 1.9 万亿美元，特斯拉市值约
1.5 万亿美元。
将上述资产整合于同⼀屋檐下的核⼼逻辑，在于双⽅共同的战略雄⼼：跨越太空、⽆⼈驾驶汽
⻋与⼈形机器⼈三⼤领域，全⾯布局⼈⼯智能。
SpaceX 正式名称为 Space Exploration Technologies Corp. ，总部位于得克萨斯州星际基地

（ Starbase ）。该公司于今年 6 ⽉在纽约完成具有⾥程碑意义的⾸次公开募股，此次上市也令⻢
斯克成为全球⾸位⾝家突破万亿美元的富豪。近年来， SpaceX 已从领先的⽕箭制造商和发射服
务商，转型为向 Anthropic PBC 等企业提供 AI 基础设施的关键供应商，同时也是全球顶级的太空
互联⽹服务提供商。

Document 459: インバウンド消費の動向（2026年7-9月期）－客数減でも消費額は7-9月期最高、台湾が首位に立つ「主役交代」

Source PDF: all report pdf/インバウンド消費の動向（2026年7-9月期）－客数減でも消費額は7-9月期最高、台湾が首位に立つ「主役交代」.pdf
Report source: NLI
Date: 2026-11-12
Pages: 10
Status: ok

### Page 1

1――はじめに～訪日客数は２期連続減も消費額は伸び率回復、近隣市場と単価が下支え

前稿1では、2026年4-6月期について、中国の６割減に加え、イラン情勢の影響などで欧州からの訪
日客も減少に転じ、訪日外客数がコロナ禍後初めて前年割れ（前年同期比▲5.3％）となる一方、単価
の上昇に支えられて消費額は前年同期をわずかに上回り（同＋0.3％2）、第２四半期として過去最高を
更新した状況を報告した。あわせて、近隣市場や成長市場による下支えと、中国からの訪日客が高単
価で買い物志向の強い層へと絞り込まれている変化を指摘し、2026年通年では客数は前年を下回る一
方、消費額は前年をわずかに上回るとの試算を示した。 
2026年7-9月期も、訪日客数と消費の動きが分かれる構図が続いた。訪日外客数は964万人（前年
同期比▲4.8％）と２四半期連続で前年を下回った一方、消費額は２兆2,076億円（同＋3.2％）と7-
9月期として過去最高を更新した。１人当たり旅行支出が 23.4万円（同＋6.1％）へと上昇して客数
の減少を補った形であり、前期にほぼ横ばいだった消費額の伸び率も持ち直した。 
９月には中東情勢に伴う航空運賃の上昇などで多くの市場が前年を下回ったが、韓国・台湾・香港
といった近隣市場は２～３割の高い伸びを示し、消費額では台湾が首位となった。中国の消費額が４
割減となるなかでも全体の増加が保たれた背景には、近隣市場の伸びと単価の上昇による下支えがあ
ると考えられる。 
本稿では、観光庁「インバウンド消費動向調査」の最新値（2026年7-9月期）をもとにインバウン
ド消費の実態を分析し、2026年通年の訪日客数・消費額の見通しについて前稿の試算を検証する。

1 久我尚子「インバウンド消費の動向（2026年 4-6月期）～訪日客数減でも消費額は維持、「量から質」への転換が現実
に」、ニッセイ基礎研究所、基礎研レポート（2026/07/30） 
2 2026年 4-6月期の消費額は、その後公表された２次速報では２兆 5,125億円（前年同期比＋0.3％）となっている（１次
速報：２兆 5,096億円、同＋0.2％）。本稿で 4-6月期の値に言及する場合は２次速報値を用いる。 
2026-11-12 
基礎研 
レポート

2――訪日外客数～２期連続の前年割れ、近隣市場は好調も９月は多くの市場で減少

入国外国人旅行者のこと。駐在員やその家族、留学生等の入国者・再入国者は訪日外客に含まれる。

### Page 3

▲4.8％となり、２四半期連続の前年割れとなった（図表１）。月別にみると、７月は前年同月比＋0.2％
と４カ月ぶりに前年を上回ったものの、８月（同▲9.6％）、９月（同▲5.0％）は再び前年を下回った。
1-9月の累計は3,073万人（前年同期比▲2.9％）と、前年を下回る水準で推移している。 
国籍・地域別にみると、最多は韓国（258万7,515人、全体の26.8％）であり、次いで台湾（202万
5,406人、同 21.0％）、中国（127万 7,096人、同 13.2％）、米国（70万 1,080人、同 7.3％）、香港
（66万4,795人、同6.9％）が続いた（図表２）。

中国の構成比は前年同期（27.3％）と比べて半分以下に低下し、前年同期の首位から今期は韓国と
台湾を下回る３位となった。一方、韓国（前年同期比＋28.7％）、香港（同＋20.5％）、台湾（同＋15.6％）
は２～３割の増加となり、中国の減少分（▲149万人）の約３分の２を、この３市場の増加分（＋96
万人）が補った形である。 
そのほかの主要国・地域では、メキシコ（同＋20.5％）とロシア（同＋12.4％）が１割を超える増
加となり、シンガポール（同＋9.5％）、スペイン（同＋9.2％）、インド（同＋8.1％）、イタリア（同
＋7.4％）も５％を超える増加となった。また、フランス（同＋3.7％）、マレーシア（同＋2.4％）、イ
ンドネシア（同＋2.1％）、英国（同＋2.0％）も前年をやや上回った。 
一方で、フィリピン（同▲11.3％）が１割超の減少となったほか、ベトナム（同▲6.4％）、ドイツ
（同▲6.0％）、豪州（同▲5.0％）、タイ（同▲3.3％）、カナダ（同▲2.1％）でも前年を下回った。米
国（同＋0.7％）はほぼ横ばいにとどまった。 
こうした国・地域による増減の分かれ方に
は、大きく２つの要因が影響しているとみら
れる。 
第一に、中東情勢に伴う航空便への影響が
続いていることである。前稿で指摘した通り、
2026年２月末以降、中東系航空会社の主要路
線の欠航により中東ハブ経由で日本と欧州を
結ぶルートが制限され、燃油サーチャージを
含む航空運賃も上昇している。日本政府観光
局によれば、９月は中東情勢に伴う航空券価
格の上昇や台風による航空便の欠航などが影
響し、多くの市場で前年同月を下回った4。実
際、欧州や豪州、カナダ、東南アジアの一部などでは、９月に前年割れとなった市場が目立つ（図表
３）。 
一方、休暇時期のずれというカレンダー要因で４月に減少していたイタリアやスペインでは、７月・
８月は前年を１割以上上回っており、遠距離市場の訪日需要そのものが崩れたわけではないとみられ

4 日本政府観光局「訪日外客数（2026年９月推計値）」（2026/10/21）。同発表では、９月は愛知県で開催されたアジア競技
大会に伴う選手や関係者の訪日が下支えとなった一方、台風による航空便の欠航や中東情勢に伴う航空券価格の上昇など
が影響したとしている。

る。ただし、航空運賃の上昇が長引く場合には、こうした市場の伸びが抑えられる可能性がある点に
は留意が必要である。 
第二に、前年の反動や一時的な要因を含む近隣市場の動きである。香港からの訪日客は前年７月に
大きく減少しており5、今年７月の大幅増（前年同月比＋54.8％）にはその反動が含まれる。また、
９月には愛知県でアジア競技大会が開催され、選手や関係者の訪日が下支えとなった面もあるとされ
る。もっとも、韓国は今期を通じて前年同月比２割を超える増加が続き、台湾も各月で前年を上回っ
ており、近隣市場の訪日需要は、こうした要因を除いても底堅いといえる。 
前期に続き、今期の増加を支えたのは、韓国・台湾・香港といった東アジアの近隣市場である。近
距離で航空運賃の上昇の影響を相対的に受けにくいとみられる近隣市場が、中国減と遠距離市場の伸
び悩みを補う構図が鮮明になっている。四半期ごとに伸びをけん引する市場は入れ替わりながらも、
全体として訪日需要の底堅さが保たれている様子がうかがえる。 
とはいえ、訪日客数全体では２四半期連続で前年を下回っており、中国という最大市場の穴を埋め
きれていない状況に変わりはない。中国からの訪日客は、2025年 11月の渡航自粛の呼びかけ以降、
前年同月比で５割前後の減少が続いている（図表４）。９月は▲44.5％と減少幅がやや縮小したが、前
年９月の水準が前後の月と比べて低かったことによる面が大きく、回復に向かう兆しはまだみられな
い。中国の渡航自粛の呼びかけが長期化する場合や、中東情勢による航空運賃の上昇が長引く場合に
は、減少が続く可能性もある。多様化は減少を和らげる緩衝材として機能し続けているものの、量的
な拡大を取り戻すには至っていない。 
なお、2026年通年の訪日客数について、前稿の試算を更新した。今回は1-9月の実績を踏まえ、中
国からの訪日客数と中国以外からの訪日客数について、それぞれ 10月以降も 1-9月と同程度の前年
同期比増減率で推移すると仮定した。この場合、2026年の訪日客数全体は約 4,210万人と、2025年
（4,268万人）を約55万人下回る水準である（図表５）。1-9月の実績がすでに前年を約90万人下回
っていることを踏まえると、これまで拡大基調が続いてきた訪日客数は、2026年に減少へ転じる公算

5 2025年７月の香港からの訪日客数は前年同月比▲36.9％。同月に日本で地震が発生するとの情報が拡散されたことや、台
風による航空便への影響などが要因とされる（トラベルボイス「訪日外国人数、７月は 344万人、東南アジア 6市場で前
年割れ、香港は 37％減、酷暑の影響も」2025/8/20）。
